*Session Aug 13 2026 (continuing the same session — last of the 5-item placement roadmap, Inventory tab piece) — added Magic Item Attunement (`sheet/index.html`, `items`/`character_sheet` schema): new "Attunement (N/3)" box under the Equipped Gear doll, showing only currently-equipped items flagged `items.requires_attunement`, with Attune/Unattune buttons and a 3-item cap. Checked for parallel-branch overlap first (standing workflow) and found real, heavy overlap in `dm.html`'s item-form region specifically — deferred just the DM Tools checkbox (no item is flagged attunement-requiring yet, so nothing is blocked); built everything else, which lives entirely in `sheet/index.html` with zero overlap. Real bug caught and fixed before shipping, not just a clean build: the handoff's suggested "clear attunement on unequip" hook needed adding at 4 places an item leaves `EQUIPPED` (found a 4th the handoff didn't mention, `autoUnequipOffHandForTwoHanded()`), but one of the 3 the handoff implied — `selectEquipItem()`'s same-item slot-move path — would have wrongly cleared attunement on a same-item slot move (e.g. moving a ring left→right hand) rather than an actual unequip; caught via careful tracing before writing the call, not via testing after. Used `EQUIPPED_ITEMS` (always a full item row for equipped items) instead of the handoff's suggested `CATALOG_CACHE` (a mixed cache — partial rows from item search, full rows from inventory fetch), since attunement specifically needs `requires_attunement`, a field the partial rows never carry. Verified end-to-end on Kael with 3 temporarily-flagged real items (reverted after): attune-to-cap, cap correctly blocks a 4th, unequip correctly clears attunement, slot-move correctly preserves it (the exact bug the fix prevents), zero console errors, zero mobile overflow. This closes the original 5-item roadmap — Cantrips & Prepared Spells remains as its own separate, larger session per the original scope note. See Decision Log for full detail.*

*Session Aug 13 2026 (continuing the same working session from Aug 12 — third of the 5-item placement roadmap, Story tab piece) — added Speed + Size to the Your Background accordion (`sheet/index.html`, `species` schema), as a new "Speed & Size" card next to the existing Home Region card. Two real open questions resolved with the DM before writing anything, per the handoff's own instruction not to guess either: (1) placement — the handoff's two suggested options (header triple-row vs. "Who You Are" accordion) both mismatched its own stated reasoning ("near Species/Region"); DM picked a third option, Your Background (where the real Region card already lives) — the better literal fit. (2) rules edition — the handoff's own example ("Dwarves are 25 ft") was 2014 PHB, but this campaign's confirmed basis elsewhere in the code is 2024 PHB; DM confirmed 2024. Checked `species` schema first (no `speed_ft`/`size` columns existed) and cross-verified the 2024 PHB speed/size table against multiple web sources before seeding real gameplay data from memory — confirmed Dwarf/Gnome/Halfling all moved to 30 ft (from 25 ft), Goliath is the one 35 ft standout, Gnome/Halfling stay Small, everyone else Medium. Seeded all 12 real species via migration, keyed off the existing `phb_equivalent` column. Side-finding, not acted on: `species.phb_equivalent` already exists and could replace the hardcoded `SPECIES_PHB` JS mirror added in an earlier session — flagged, not fixed (out of scope here). Verified live on Kael (Veilstepped → Changeling → 30 ft/Medium): card reads "30 FT · MEDIUM", positioned correctly next to Home Region, zero console errors, zero mobile overflow. Pushed live. See Decision Log for full detail.*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (same session, second of the 5-item placement roadmap — Header piece) — added Initiative + Hit Dice to the MainInfo header (`sheet/index.html`), next to AC/HP/Temp HP in `.cs-mi-vitals`. Initiative (`DEX mod + 5 if Alert feat`) needed no async refresh — renders once, correctly, in the initial synchronous header build, since it only depends on `ABILITY_SCORES`/`ch.feats`, both already resolved by then. Verified live on Kael (a real Alert-feat case): DEX +2 + Alert +5 = **+7**, correct. Hit Dice: mid-build, discovered via the required "confirm the DB write" verification step that `character_sheet` already had unused `hit_dice_total`/`hit_dice_used` columns (populated `1`/`0` on all 3 characters, zero code references anywhere) — the handoff's premise of "no existing column" was wrong. Had already added a new `hit_dice_spent` column on that premise; dropped it and consolidated onto the pre-existing `hit_dice_used` instead, rather than ship two live "spent" trackers side by side. `hit_dice_total` stays dead/unused — max is deliberately computed live from `ch.level` per the handoff's own don't-cache-max instruction, not read from that column. Verified end-to-end: toggled Kael's pip, confirmed the write via SQL, toggled back, confirmed it reverted to 0. Zero console errors, zero mobile overflow (wraps cleanly onto its own line at narrow widths). Pushed live. See Decision Log for full detail — this is the more consequential entry of the two on record today, given the mid-build schema correction.*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (same session, first of a 5-item Class/Header/Story/Inventory placement roadmap agreed with DM — this handoff was just the Class tab piece) — added Spellcasting Modifier/Spell Save DC/Spell Attack Bonus to the Class tab (`sheet/index.html`), new card directly above Spell Slots. Investigated the data source first per the handoff's own instruction not to guess: confirmed `classes` had no structured spellcasting-ability column, and `primary_ability` is unreliable for half-casters (Paladin "Strength & Charisma", Ranger "Dexterity & Wisdom" — the real casting ability is only recoverable from `casting_summary`'s free text). Flagged this to the DM via a question rather than picking silently; DM chose "add a real column." Added `classes.spellcasting_ability` (str/dex/con/int/wis/cha, null for non-casters) via migration, seeded all 12 real classes from their `casting_summary` text (matches standard 5e rules exactly). Found mid-build that the handoff's assumed "DM Tools content manager" companion field doesn't apply — `dm.html` has no class-editing UI at all; the `classes` table has always been maintained via direct migration only (same as `hit_die_tip`/`saves_tip`/`weapons_tip` before it) — followed that existing precedent rather than building a new admin UI unprompted. Card hides entirely for non-casters (`display:none`, not an empty/zeroed state). Refresh wired to `renderLivePlay()`, not the handoff's suggested `renderClassTabDynamic()` hook — same deviation as the Saves build, for the same reason (`renderClassTabDynamic()` fires before `SHEET` loads). Verified live on Kael Evander (Ranger, real half-caster case): Spellcasting Modifier +3, Spell Save DC 13, Spell Attack Bonus +5, all hand-checked against his real WIS 16 and proficiency bonus 2. Simulated the non-caster path in-browser (no data touched) — card hides correctly, restores correctly. Zero console errors, zero mobile overflow. Pushed live. See Decision Log for full detail.*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (same session, addendum to the Saves/Passive Perception build below) — added the one item that build left open: a top-level "what's a saving throw?" explainer (`sheet/index.html`). DM's decision: one explainer, not six (the six `SAVE_TIPS` stay as-is, they explain what triggers each save specifically) — and since saves render one-per-card with no shared "Saving Throws" heading to hang a single tooltip off of, DM left the anchor choice to Claude Code. Attached it to STR's row only, as a new `SAVE_INTRO_TIP` shown via a second `.cs-ab-tip` icon on that row alone — styled distinctly (blue "i" pill, new `.cs-ab-tip--intro` class, vs. the standard gold "?") so it reads as a general concept explainer, not a Strength-specific fact. `abilitySaveRowHtml()` took a new `showIntroTip` param, `true` only when `ab === 'str'`, at both call sites (initial render and the `renderAbilitySkills()` refresh). No other changes — this was the only remaining open item from the original handoff. Verified live on Kael: intro tip present only on STR's row (confirmed via DOM read, not just visual), correct copy, zero console errors. Pushed live. See Decision Log for full detail.*

*Session Aug 12 2026 (same session, after the PHB-terms fix below) — added Saving Throws + Passive Perception to the Ability Scores tab (`sheet/index.html`), per a DM handoff doc. One row per ability card ("Save", reusing the existing skill-row visual family — same "Prof." label, same positive/negative coloring) sourced from `classes.saving_throws` (a comma-separated string, e.g. "Strength, Dexterity" — confirmed against all 12 real classes before writing the parser) via a new `parseSavingThrows()`. Passive Perception (`10 + Perception total`) renders as a small gold-bordered pill on the WIS card only, above its Save row — extracted a new `computeSkillTotal()` out of the existing skill-row renderer so the skill list and the passive badge share one proficiency/flat-bonus calculation instead of two. Both refresh once `SHEET.proficiency_bonus` and `CLASS_ROW.saving_throws` are guaranteed loaded (`renderAbilitySkills()`, already the right timing hook — `CLASS_ROW` resolves before `renderLivePlay()` calls it, confirmed from the real fetch-chain order, not assumed). No `css/tokens.css` changes — reused existing tokens (`--gold`, `--radius-sm`, etc.) directly. Left one thing explicitly undecided per the handoff doc's own instruction not to guess DM's-call items: no top-level "what's a saving throw?" explainer beyond the per-ability tooltips — flagging for DM review rather than picking a tone unilaterally (resolved same session, see entry above).   New tokens: `--blue-50/100/400/600/800`, `--char-ov-h5` (18px).

Removed the header's weight bar (not in Figma; Inventory tab already had its own independent "Carrying: X lb" line, so nothing was lost) — deleted `renderOverviewWeight()` and its markup/CSS as dead code. AC badge rebuilt to exact values (blue/100 bg, blue/800 text, bottom-only 55px radius, split "AC" label/number typography) — was a single uniform gold pill. HP row rebuilt: current AND max are both real `<input type="number">` fields, no Edit toggle, no −/+/Apply/Set-Max-Mod panel — editing max derives `hp_max_modifier` from the typed value minus the real `hp_max` base (DM's explicit call, replacing the separate Max-Mod button rather than adding a third field). Temp HP gets the same direct-input treatment and **no longer hides at 0** — a deliberate reversal of the earlier "hides entirely at 0/null" decision, since a directly-typeable field needs to always be present to type into; flagged to the DM as an implied consequence of switching interaction models, not silently changed.

The 6th field (`personality_laugh`) isn't in that Figma frame at all — flagged to the DM rather than inventing copy; DM supplied eyebrow "Laugh" / title "What Always Makes You Laugh" directly, confirmed landing verbatim (not a paraphrase) before committing. "YOUR STORY" page title added above Who You Are, then to Ability Scores/Inventory/Class too once confirmed none of the four tabs had one (contrary to the initial ask's premise) — one shared `pageTitleHtml()` helper. Icon thumbnails added to all 7 Your Past cards and 3 of 4 Your Background cards (Gigglegloom Affinity/Home Region/Background — not Moral Alignment, which has no image anywhere in the codebase to source from, confirmed by reading `character.html`, not fabricated), all verbatim from the exact asset paths `character.js`/`character.html` already use for these same option cards. Skill-proficiency benefit tags got `?` tooltips via new `SKILL_TIPS` (mirrored from `character.js`). Identity pills row removed (redundant with the blurb, not in Figma) along with the now-dead `SPECIES_PHB` lookup. Condition pills reordered to render after "+ Add Condition" per the mockup. Moral Alignment card fixed to pull from a new `ALIGNMENT_CARD_DESC` (real second-person text verbatim from `character.html`'s `.char-alignment-desc`) instead of the third-person `ALIGNMENT_TRAIT_SENTENCE` meant for the header blurb — wrong source, not a wording problem. **DM-reviewed follow-up**: Temp HP had briefly grown a decorative always-100%-filled bar (added to visually match the new AC/HP compact badges) — reverted to a plain number badge on DM instruction, since 5e has no real "max temp HP" to show a bar or proportion against; still correctly hides entirely at 0/null. Verified live on Kael and Visenya (different gigglegloom/region/background/alignment combinations each), zero console errors, before/after via `git stash`.
- **Structural cleanup pass, same session, after the tab shell landed** (`sheet/index.html`): four fixes requested together, all low-risk relocations/bug fixes, no redesign. (1) Gold/Equipped/Inventory/Starting Gear were still rendering inside Your Story — leftover from before the tab redesign, never relocated when Inventory got its own tab. Moved all four to the Inventory tab verbatim (same ids, same `renderGold()`/`renderEquipped()`/`renderInventory()` calls) so Inventory has real working content instead of a placeholder. (2) Proficient Skills moved from Your Past to Ability Scores — its real conceptual home, flagged as homeless when it was first built. (3) `buildSummaryCardHtml()` was dumping all six `generateSummary()` sections (Who Are You/Your Magic/Your Background/Your Past/Your Appearance/Your Compass) into the Who You Are card — confirmed via a stash/screenshot before-comparison. Fixed to show only the "WHO ARE YOU" section via the existing `extractSummarySection()` helper; the other five are already covered by their own dedicated cards. (4) Appearance chips moved from between the Your Background/Your Past accordions to directly after Who You Are, so it groups with the rest of the who-you-are content instead of floating between two unrelated accordions — exact placement flagged as DM's call to adjust after seeing it live. **Follow-up, DM-requested after reviewing the above**: the portrait card's Copper/Silver/Gold split-entry boxes (`renderOverviewMoney()`/`onOverviewMoneyChange()`) turned out to be a second live editor for the exact same `character_sheet.gold_cp` field the new Inventory-tab Gold section (`renderGold()`/`setGold()`) already owns — confirmed by reading both functions, not assumed. They were built to stay in sync when both lived on the same page (Aug 7 session); now that Gold has its own tab, keeping two editors for one field was pure duplication, not two different features. Removed the header boxes entirely (markup, both functions, both call sites, the now-dead `cs-ov-money-*` CSS) — `renderGold()`/`setGold()` in the Inventory tab remain the single editor. Left the now-unused `--currency-copper/silver/gold-coin` tokens in `tokens.css` (not asked, and tokens.css tolerates defined-but-unused tokens without harm). Verified end-to-end on Kael (before/after via `git stash`) and spot-checked Visenya's old-format-summary fallback still degrades to no card, both with zero console errors.
- **Hana and Visenya's `player_characters.summary` are still old-format** (confirmed via SQL: both 186 chars with no "YOUR APPEARANCE" section; Kael's is 1530 chars with all six sections — Kael's was regenerated post-rewrite, theirs weren't). This matters now because the header's appearance paragraph (below) reads from this field: Hana and Visenya will show the `buildAppearanceDesc(app)` fallback style (choppier, fragment-based — "Tall, lean build. Adult. Heart face...") instead of Kael's flowing generateSummary() prose until their summaries are regenerated. Not fixed — DM's explicit call to leave it on record rather than act on it now.
- **"Your Past" label maps rebuilt in full** (`sheet/index.html`) — all 5 maps flagged as stale last check-in (`RAISED_LABELS`, `PET_LABELS`, `LOVE_LABELS`, `ORG_LABELS`, `LEFT_BEHIND_LABELS`) now resolve every real `character.html` option value to its exact authored card text, not sheet's own earlier abbreviated guesses or the cap+dash-replace fallback. New `FRIEND_LABELS` added — `dearest_friend` never had a map at all before, always rendered the raw stored slug. All dead/unused keys dropped (26 of them, combined, across the old 5 maps). Verified before/after on Kael and Visenya via live DOM reads before committing, not assumed from the diff.
- **Header appearance paragraph fixed** (`sheet/index.html`) — was showing `appearance_prompt` verbatim (the raw AI-portrait-generation prompt string, e.g. "Fantasy portrait of, average height, athletic, adult, with #ffba66 skin..."), now shows the "YOUR APPEARANCE" section extracted from the same `generateSummary()` text already used in Who You Are (new `extractSummarySection()`, built on a factored-out `parseSummaryBlocks()` shared with `buildSummaryCardHtml()` so the two can't drift). Falls back to `buildAppearanceDesc(app)` when a character's summary predates the six-section rewrite (see the Hana/Visenya bullet above). Of the three original appearance-duplication copies (header paragraph / chips / Who You Are narrative), this actually reduces the duplication rather than just relocating it: the header and Who You Are now show the *same* text (when a character has a new-format summary) instead of two independently-worded retellings — down to two distinct versions (that shared text, plus the chips) from three.
- **`dm.html` Combat tab "Add Condition" modal** (`sccPromptCondition()`, third and last instance of the hardcoded-conditions gap): had its own separate local `COND_DATA` — 15 core conditions only, no Muted/Fading Stage 1/Color-Drained/Exhaustion, never referenced `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA` even after the Live Session panel fix earlier this session. Replaced entirely with `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA` (already fetched once in `sccInit()`) — the modal now lists all 18 real conditions with real colors/tooltips. No local fallback data left in this function at all, unlike the Live Session panel (which still has `COND_DATA` as a legacy fast-path plus this table as the fallback) — this modal only ever had the one data source to begin with, so full replacement was the right call, not a hybrid.
- **Inspiration badge tooltip + tooltip viewport clamping** (`sheet/index.html`): the Inspiration badge never got a `data-tip` — added the DM's exact rules text ("Spend before any d20 roll — roll twice, take the higher result. You can only hold one at a time.") and a `wireAllTooltips()` call inside `renderInspiration()` itself (it wasn't calling it before; relied on the caller to, which `toggleInspiration()` didn't). Testing this surfaced a real, previously-invisible bug in the shared `wireTooltip()` mechanism: it centers the bubble on the element with no viewport-edge awareness, and the Inspiration badge sits in the header's literal top-right corner (per the earlier header-alignment fix) — the bubble overflowed off-screen, unreadable. Fixed in `wireTooltip()` itself (not special-cased for Inspiration), clamping the bubble's center so it can't render closer than 8px to either viewport edge. This is a shared fix — every `data-tip` element in the file benefits, not just Inspiration; confirmed via the math that it's a no-op for elements not near an edge (the clamp only engages when the unclamped position would overflow).
- **Sheet Conditions picker** (`sheet/index.html`, follow-up to the `dm.html` SCC fix below): the MainInfo header's Conditions row was free-text (type anything, generic purple pill, no tooltip) since the slice-1 header build — flagged then as a known gap ("no canonical color source"), no longer true now that the `conditions` Supabase table exists. Replaced with a picker: new `fetchConditionsData()` (`sb.from('conditions').select('*').order('sort_order')`, fetched once in the same init `Promise.all` as `fetchBackgroundData`/`fetchRegionData`/etc.) populates `CONDITIONS_DATA`; `renderConditions()` now renders a `<select>` of conditions not already applied instead of a text input, and each pill's colors come from real `color_bg`/`color_border`/`color_text` (CSS custom properties per pill, same `--cond-bg`/`--cond-border`/`--cond-text` technique `dm.html`'s SCC Live Session panel uses) with real `tip` text wired through the existing `wireAllTooltips()` system built earlier this session — conditions now get real hover tooltips for the first time. Removed the now-inaccurate CSS comment claiming conditions have no color source. Testing also surfaced (not introduced, not fixed) a latent race in the existing `saveSheet()`-per-click pattern: two `removeCondition()` calls fired with zero delay between them (only happened in my own test script, calling the function directly twice in a row) can let an earlier PATCH complete after a later one and leave stale data in Supabase. Confirmed real click-driven usage — any perceptible delay between actions — doesn't hit this, and the fire-and-forget `saveSheet()` pattern itself is unchanged from the old free-text flow, so this is a pre-existing risk, not something this edit introduced. Left as a noted risk rather than fixed unprompted.
- **SCC Live Session conditions — added "Muted"** (`dm.html`): `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` now has 17 entries (was 16). More importantly, fixed the underlying gap this surfaced — `sccLiveRenderConditions()`'s `COND_DATA` is a fully local, hardcoded object covering only the 15 core D&D conditions; "Fading Stage 1" and "Color-Drained" were already in the picker list but had no entry in `COND_DATA`, so they silently fell through to a generic purple placeholder with no real tooltip (just the condition's own name). Rather than add a third hardcoded copy of Muted's color/tip (a real `conditions` table already exists in Supabase, seeded same-day, `is_custom:true` for the 3 Anavale-specific ones), added a `conditions` fetch to `sccInit()`'s existing `Promise.all` (same pattern as `species`/`items`) into `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA`, and changed the lookup chain to `COND_DATA[c] || sccConditionsTableLookup(c) || genericFallback`. This fixes Muted **and** retroactively fixes Fading Stage 1/Color-Drained's missing real tooltips as a side effect of the same code path — not scope creep, the fix the DM asked for necessarily covered all three. Left the separate, unrelated `COND_DATA` in `sccPromptCondition()` (combat-tab "Add Condition" modal, line ~12421) untouched — different feature, not mentioned, out of scope. Verified: picker dropdown now lists Muted; added Muted + Fading Stage 1 + Poisoned to a test player, confirmed all three render with correct real colors/tooltips (Muted and Fading Stage 1 from Supabase, Poisoned still from local `COND_DATA` — correct precedence), screenshotted, then cleared the test state (`SCC_LIVE_CONDITIONS` is pure client-side in-memory state, confirmed via grep it's never written to Supabase, so no DB cleanup was needed).
- `sheet/index.html` now has a 4-button tab bar (Your Story / Ability Scores / Inventory / Class) with working show/hide. All four tabs hold real content now — Inventory's is a stopgap (Gold/Equipped/Inventory/Starting Gear relocated there unstyled, see the Aug 9 tab-cleanup entry below), still waiting on its real Figma pass.
- **MainInfo header alignment fix** (post-slice-4, DM-requested after the header/Figma mismatch below was flagged): re-pulled node `51:1385` directly (higher-res single-component screenshot, clearer than the full-page `51:1103` pull) and corrected exactly the three things the DM named, nothing else. Conditions now render as inline pills in a `.cs-mi-top-row` above the name (was below Appearance); Inspiration is now a small circular badge (`.cs-mi-insp-badge`, 64px, dashed border, gold-filled when active) absolutely positioned top-right of `.cs-ov-maininfo` (was a pill button next to the AC badge — `.cs-ov-maininfo` needed `position:relative` added for this to anchor); Species/Appearance/Speaks are now a labeled 3-column `.cs-mi-triple-row` (was three stacked full-width blocks) — Speaks lost its boxed-pill styling to read as a plain column like the other two. Condition pills use `--purple`/`--purple-dim`/`--purple-border` (existing tokens) rather than inventing a per-condition-name color scheme — conditions are arbitrary free text with no canonical color source, Figma's mockup colors (pink "Charmed", green "Poisoned") aren't derivable from real data. AC badge/HP bar/Temp HP/death-save swap deliberately untouched — DM confirmed those already matched. Verified via before/after screenshots on Kael (with test conditions + Inspiration toggled on to confirm both pill and active-badge styling render, then reverted) and a clean spot-check on Visenya.
- **Your Story tab** (final slice, built against a real Figma screenshot pulled via `get_screenshot` on node `51:1103` — the written spec's "6 chips" description didn't say whether these were small pills or wider cards, and the real answer data runs up to full paragraphs, so pulling the actual frame settled it: wide description cards, not pills): three accordions — **Who You Are** (Summary paragraph + 6 cards: First Impression/Misunderstood/Makes You Laugh/Care About/Fear/Quest, the last 3 relocated from the old flat story-row list, the first 3 new fields never shown before); **Your Background** (Gigglegloom/Home Region/Alignment/Background cards — Home Region is new, joins the `regions` table on `home_region` for `regions.summary`; Background's Benefits box parses `backgrounds.bonuses` via `parseBackgroundAbilityBonuses()`/`getBackgroundFeat()`, ported verbatim from `character.js` per the spec, plus `backgrounds.skills` as proficiency tags); **Your Past** (7 cards — Upbringing/Friend/Pet/Love/Organization/Left Behind/Why You Left, Benefits boxes filter `player_characters.skill_bonuses` by its `past_raised:`/`past_friend:`/`past_org:` source-tag prefixes, confirmed against real data before writing the filter). This **entirely replaces** the old pre-redesign "Overview" accordion (alignment/background/gigglegloom cards + narrative summary) — that accordion's cards move into Your Background, the summary paragraph moves into Who You Are, and the Ability-Scores-slice feat chip that landed on the old Background card moves into the new Background card's Benefits box, completing what that slice's Decision Log entry flagged as transitional.
- Two things resolved by DM decision before this slice: **"Why You Left" personal quest line** — no column existed for it; built as a static `WHY_LEFT_QUEST` mirror (DM's call), sourced verbatim from `character.html`'s real `data-effect` card attributes (only 5 real option values exist there — `ran-from-something`/`received-message`/`restlessness`/`saw-the-grey`/`someone-disappeared`; the existing `WHY_LEFT_LABELS` mirror has extra keys that don't correspond to any real character-creator option, a pre-existing staleness already flagged in the Aug 7 log, not touched here). **Spell Slots** — DM-approved minimal stopgap, see below.

"Proficient skills" (class-chosen, distinct from the flat +1 `skill_bonuses`) kept as a small standalone row under the Your Past cards — no new home specified anywhere, data preserved rather than dropped.

Shape is `{level: count}`, invented here since nothing existed to match. Clicking a filled pip spends it, clicking an empty one restores up to it — same interaction as the death-save pips. Verified end-to-end on Visenya then reverted the test values.
- Class tab: class summary card + Hit Die/Primary/Saves/Casting/Armor/Tools/Weapons (Hit Die is a new row; `?` tooltips on Hit Die/Saves/Weapons only) + Key Features (icon table ported verbatim from `character.js`, but the name/description/level parser is genuinely new code — verified against all 72 real feature strings in Supabase before writing). Photo upload (`cs-hero-portrait-img` / `cs-photo-input` ids preserved) kept working on the new portrait card per DM confirmation.

Storage format: plain text, `LABEL\n\nparagraph`, sections joined by `\n\n\n`. Live preview element `char-auto-summary` changed from `<p>` to `<div>` (character.html) so multi-paragraph `innerHTML` can render; new `.char-summary-heading` CSS added to `character.css`.

**Next priorities:**
- **Needs a real-browser confirmation, not a code check** — the Live Session strip's `position:sticky` left column (built 2026-08-19). This session's own browser tooling could not visually confirm sticky positioning at all (reproduced the same failure on a fully isolated, unrelated test element), so the underlying CSS was fixed via direct spec-based reasoning and computed-style checks, not by watching it actually stick. Have the DM open the Session tab, expand any accordion section with enough content to scroll, and confirm the left column (Party HP/Conditions/Current Beat/etc.) visibly stays in place while the right column scrolls past it.
- **BUG — character photo Replace upload fails with "Upload failed — The resource already exists."** Reported by Hema, on the character photo Replace flow (`sheet/index.html`). Not yet investigated. Worth checking first, not assumed: this error text is the classic signature of an `insert`-style storage upload hitting a path that already exists, rather than an `upsert` against the `character-photos` bucket — verify against the real upload code before acting on this guess.
- **Combat encounter persistence** — no schema exists for pre-staging an encounter (Setup) or reviewing one after the fact (history). These are one plan→run→resolve lifecycle, not two separate projects; the real minimum is persisting what `sccEndCombat()`'s Combat Summary popup already computes instead of discarding it. Closes the XP/loot session-tagging gap too (`quest_progress.xp_awarded`/`loot_awarded`, `session_notes.inspiration_awarded`/`session_summary` are all confirmed dead columns).
- `quest_progress` has no durable session history — a beat's `completed_in_session` is a single overwritable stamp, not a log. Needs a real append-only log table if "which session did X complete in" is ever meant to be a durable fact. Medium priority, nothing actively broken today.
- **Creature/NPC stat-block unification** — two independent renderers (Combat's own, the lookup drawer's) still exist; same risk shape as the already-completed NPC/Location field-union.
- **Attunement checkbox in the DM Tools item edit form** (`dm.html`) for the real `requires_attunement` field — confirmed still missing via grep. Schema, sheet-side UI, and toggle logic already shipped; this is the one remaining piece.
- **A DM Tools admin form for `classes.limited_use_features`** (add/remove a class's limited-use features, edit the level→max table) — confirmed still missing via grep; currently DM-edited via direct migration only, same precedent as `spell_slot_progression`/`prepared_count_formula`.
- **Known, unaddressed gap — quests have no path to players at all.** All real quests are `player_facing:false` and `js/router.js`/`js/wiki.js` have zero quest-route code — there is no public wiki page a quest could link to even if marked visible. Blocks Search+Preview's "open in new tab" for quests and any real DM/player-facing split for the Quest Manager. Needs an explicit decision (a real `#quest` route + renderer? a DM-only shareable view instead?) before this can move.
- @mention predictive entity search in notes — confirmed genuinely new work, no existing pattern to crib from (the existing predictive-search implementations are all single-line inputs, none do caret-aware inline insertion into a textarea).
- Cross-session Tagged Note search — everything is still scoped to one session, a real gap once a second session exists.
- 4 possibly-non-core spell flags (`cause-fear`, `aganazzar-s-scorcher`, `abi-dalzim-s-horrid-wilting`, `tasha-s-otherworldly-guise`) need a DM decision — not yet acted on.
- 5 duplicate spell catalog entries (same real spell stored under both its 2014/trademarked name and its 2024-SRD/renamed equivalent) — deduping is a structural decision outside the audit's own scope, needs a DM call.
- Tier 1 audit (level/school/classes) of the 41 already-flagged-unavailable spells — lowest priority, unavailable to any character regardless of correctness.
- Point-pool limited-use features (Monk Focus/Ki Points, Paladin Lay on Hands, Sorcerer Sorcery Points) have no tracker yet — the segmented X-of-Y bar doesn't fit a point pool, needs its own numeric-input card variant.
- Bardic Inspiration has no limited-use tracker either — its max uses is Charisma-modifier-derived, not level-derived, so it doesn't fit `classes.limited_use_features`'s `max_at_level` schema. Would need an optional `max_formula` field.
- No rest-flow (long/short rest button) exists anywhere on the sheet — Hit Dice/Death Saves/Spell Slots/Spells Prepared all restore or reset via manual clicking only.
- `species.phb_equivalent` (a real, populated column) could replace the hardcoded `SPECIES_PHB` JS mirror still in `sheet/index.html` — confirmed both still exist side by side. Candidate for a small cleanup pass.
- DM Tools has no admin UI for `classes.spell_slot_progression`/`prepared_count_formula` — both seeded via direct migration, same "edit via migration for now" pattern as every other `classes` mechanical column.
- Fix the species-portrait 404 (real fix: correct the filename pattern to `sp-{species}-{suffix}.png`).
- `sheet/index.html`: combat tab, condition tooltips, XP display — long-standing, still not built.
- `assets/images/ui/icon-x.svg`/`icon-search.svg`/`icon-arrow-dropdown.svg` are staged on disk but unwired (baked-in dark fill needs a `mask-image` recolor pass before they're usable on this page's dark theme).
- Character creator migration to Supabase `species` table
- Interactive Caparia map
- Session recap page (player-facing, post-Session 2)

## 4. Repo & Deployment

**Local path:** `Documents/DND/Anavale/anavale-wiki/`

    Documents/DND/Anavale/
    ├── anavale-wiki/          ← this repo (public git)
    ├── anavale-dm-docs/       ← private git repo (DM spoiler files)
    └── anavale-assets/        ← non-git large files (mockups, world art, etc.)

**GitHub repo:** `github.com/carlosknightiii/anavale-wiki`

**Live URLs:**
| URL | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki` | Player-facing wiki |
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/dm` | DM Tools (`anavale-dm`) |
| `https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/character` | Character creator |

**Git push sequence:**

    cd Documents/DND/Anavale/anavale-wiki
    git add -A
    git commit -m "..."
    git push

**Pre-push syntax check (Node v24 — `node --check` does not work on .html files):**

    node -e "require('fs').readFileSync('dm.html','utf8')" 2>&1

Returns nothing = safe. Throws = fix before pushing.

**CDN propagation delay:** GitHub Pages can take 5–15 minutes after a successful push. Before concluding a fix didn't land:

    curl -s "https://carlosknightiii.github.io/anavale-wiki/dm.html" | grep -c "FUNCTION_OR_STRING"

Count 0 when it should be 1+ = CDN hasn't propagated. Wait 5 minutes, re-run. Never write a new fix based on browser behavior without confirming via curl first.

**Site crash recovery:**

    git log --oneline -10
    git reset --hard <last-known-good-hash> && git push origin main --force

Use `git reset --hard`, not chained `git revert`. Chained reverts create indeterminate state.

**Git lock error:** `rm -f .git/index.lock` before retrying. Never force-push to resolve a lock.

## 5. Architecture

**Code changes → git push.** HTML, CSS, JS files only.
**Content changes → Supabase only. Never git push for content.** The `data/*.js` files are legacy seeds — not the source of truth. Never read from them in new code.

**Key files:**
| File | Notes |
|---|---|
| `dm.html` | DM Tools + SCC. Most complex file. Always stale — Rule C1 applies. |
| `character.html` | Character creator shell. Potentially stale every session. |
| `css/character.css` | Character creator styles. Potentially stale. |
| `js/character.js` | Character creator logic. Potentially stale. |
| `sheet/index.html` | Player character sheet. Potentially stale. |
| `js/wiki.js` | Wiki render logic. |
| `js/router.js` | Client-side routing. |
| `css/tokens.css` | Single source of truth for all design tokens. No `:root` blocks anywhere else. |
| `index.html` | Player-facing wiki shell. |

**The five always-stale files:** `dm.html`, `character.html`, `css/character.css`, `js/character.js`, `sheet/index.html` — read the current file state before every edit. Never assume. Never use project knowledge as a substitute for reading these files.

## 7. Debugging Rules (D1–D8)

**D1 — 2-attempt hard limit (non-negotiable).** If a fix fails twice, STOP. Do not write a third variation. Something is wrong with the diagnosis, not the fix. Say: *"I have reached the 2-attempt limit. I will not write another fix until I have read the exact current state of the broken code and identified the specific line that is wrong."*

**D2 — Diagnose before touching anything.** Read the actual broken code. Identify the exact line that is wrong and state precisely why it produces the observed behaviour. If uncertain, say so.

**D3 — Fix in one shot.** Once the root cause is identified, write one targeted fix. Do not patch symptoms.

**D4 — "Stop. Think. One fix."** When the DM writes this: halt all iteration. Re-read relevant code from scratch. State the root cause in plain English. Write one fix. Do not explain previous attempts.

**D5 — "Rewrite from first principles."** When the DM writes this: discard all accumulated edits. Write a clean version from scratch.

**D6 — Never answer without evidence.** Before answering any question about the project — code, UI, file structure, feature behavior — read the file or search project knowledge first. If the answer cannot be confirmed, say "I don't know — let me check."

**D7 — When the DM corrects a factual claim:** Stop immediately. Do not defend the previous answer. Correct the record in the Decision Log before writing any code.

**D8 — When a testing tool proves structurally unable to verify something, say so the first time, then loop in the DM immediately.** Example on record: a sandboxed browser tool that converts `target="_blank"` clicks into same-tab navigation instead of a real, inspectable new tab — no amount of retrying, different click techniques, or `window.open()` variants makes it capable of that specific check. The moment a limitation like this is confirmed (not suspected — actually tried and hit), say so plainly and ask the DM for one concrete, real verification step. Do not spend a second or third round writing another fix and re-running the same approximated/partial test as if it might now pass — a tool that structurally cannot observe the thing in question won't observe it any better on attempt three. Loop in the human check as soon as the ceiling is confirmed, not after exhausting further code changes against it.

---

## 8. Site Integrity Rules (C1–C5)

**C1 — Read dm.html before every edit. No exceptions.** Read the current state of the target function with line numbers before writing any replacement. Project knowledge is never sufficient.

**C2 — No literal emoji in JS strings.** All emoji and special characters inside JS string literals in HTML files must use `&#decimal;` entities or `\uXXXX` escapes. A single literal emoji produces `Uncaught SyntaxError: Invalid or unexpected token at dm:1:1` — crashing the entire page with no useful error message.
- Safe: `&#128203;` · `—`
- Unsafe: literal 📋 or — inside a quoted JS string

**C3 — Pre-push confirmation for dm.html.** Before any dm.html push, show the diff and confirm: no literal emoji in JS strings, no unmatched quotes.

**C4 — Site crash recovery: `git reset --hard`, not `git revert` chains.** See Section 4.

**C5 — Rejected diagnosis is confirmed wrong. Stop completely.** If the DM says a diagnosis is wrong, stop all iteration. Read the actual current code state. State the new diagnosis with the specific line and reason. Write one fix only after the new diagnosis is confirmed.

---

## 9. Code Authoring Rules

**Claude Code prompts containing code blocks:**
Always use a bash heredoc when writing file contents that include backtick code blocks. Never wrap such a prompt in a fenced code block — inner backticks will break the outer fence and content will be silently dropped.

    cat > filename.md << 'ENDOFFILE'
    ...content with any backticks, code blocks, special characters...
    ENDOFFILE

**JS strings (all files):**
- Never use literal emoji in JS strings — use `&#decimal;` or `\uXXXX` (mirrors C2)
- When running sed or regex replacements, always show the diff before committing

**CSS (`character.css`, `tokens.css`):**
- Define `:root` tokens before any component rules
- No inline `style=` attributes — all styles go in character.css
- No raw `rgba()` or hex in rules — every color references a CSS variable (exceptions: `:root` definitions and canvas JS strings)
- Semantic variable naming: `--char-card-bg` ✓ · `--char-dark-blue` ✗
- Shared elements use shared classes — no one-off cosmetic classes
- No duplicate rules — edit the existing rule, never add a second copy
- Only add a new variable when the value appears in more than one rule

**Tooltips (`character.js`, `character.html`):**
- Always `data-tip`, never `title` — `title` triggers native browser tooltip
- `initTooltips()` is an empty stub — use `wireTooltip(el)` directly
- Static HTML wires automatically on DOMContentLoaded
- Dynamic content must call `wireTooltip(el)` per element after rendering

**Positioning (`character.html`):**
- Always `position: sticky` — never JS scroll listeners for sticky behaviour
- `body.char-page` has `overflow-x: hidden` which breaks `window.scrollY` in Chrome/Safari
- Before adding any `position:fixed` or `position:absolute;inset:0` element: confirm z-index, confirm `pointer-events:none` unless interaction required, confirm it will be removed from DOM after use

**z-index ladder:**
| Element | z-index |
|---|---|
| Tooltips | 99999 |
| Confirmation overlay | 200 |
| Return banner | 100 |
| Sticky HUD (Stage 4) | 90 |
| Sticky Stage 3 panel | 50 |

**Modals (`sheet/index.html`):**
- All modals must be injected into `#cs-modal-root` — a direct sibling of `<main id="cs-main">` inside `<body>`
- Never build modals as part of `renderCharacter()`'s HTML string
- Reason: `.cs-wrap` has a `cs-fadein` animation with `forwards` fill-mode — any `animation`, `transform`, `filter`, or `perspective` on an ancestor creates a new containing block for `position:fixed` descendants, breaking them silently. Symptom: modal appears at the top of the page regardless of scroll position.

**Canvas (`welcome.html`):**
- Partition animation text is drawn via `drawText(p)` — never via HTML/CSS
- Never add HTML elements inside `#partition-sticky`

---

## 10. Design Tokens

All tokens live in `css/tokens.css` — single source of truth. No `:root` blocks anywhere else.

**Fonts (two only):**
- `--font-display: 'Cinzel Decorative', Georgia, serif` — all headings, Bold 700 only
- `--font-body: 'Roboto', system-ui, sans-serif` — all body text, labels, UI (300/400/700)
- `--font-mono: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace` — Formery stamps, code

**Aliases:** `--font-headers` → `var(--font-display)` · `--font-serif` → `var(--font-display)` · `--font-sans` → `var(--font-body)`

**Gold:** `#f0b429` everywhere. Old `#c8940a` retired. Do not re-add Charm or EB Garamond.

**Google Fonts load:** `Cinzel+Decorative:wght@700 + Roboto:ital,wght@0,300;0,400;0,700;1,300;1,400;1,700`

---

## 11. Content Policy

**Spell content policy**: Anavale only includes spells shown and as written in the official 2024 5th Edition Player's Handbook. Supplement material (Xanathar's, Tasha's, Fizban's, etc.) not reprinted in the core 2024 PHB is excluded — flagged via `spells.classes = '{}'`, never deleted (deletion is a one-way door; flagging keeps the row recoverable if this policy ever needs revisiting). When a spell exists under two names in the catalog (a trademarked/legacy name and a generic SRD name, or a 2014 name superseded by a 2024 rename), only the row matching the name/wording actually printed in the 2024 PHB stays available — the other is flagged the same way, not deleted.

This is a standing rule, not a one-off decision — a future "is this spell legit for Anavale" question should default to this policy rather than becoming a fresh DM ask each time. Only escalate to the DM when a case is genuinely ambiguous (e.g. sources disagree on what the physical 2024 PHB actually prints) — routine core-vs-supplement calls are already answered by this policy.

**2026-08-19 (same session, follow-up — Primal Order moved from a standalone Details-column note into a Key Features row, per direct DM instruction) — `sheet/index.html`.**
Supersedes item 3 of the entry directly below — the standalone `.cs-ov-class-primal-order` note under Class > Details (its own function `primalOrderNoteHtml()`, its own CSS, a "?" tooltip) is gone entirely, not kept alongside the new placement. Primal Order now renders as the first row in the Key Features list, matching the exact `.cs-class-feature-row` shape every other feature there uses (icon + name + short description, no tooltip — Key Features rows don't have tooltips anywhere else, so adding one just to this row would have been a new, unexplained inconsistency).
Real constraint driving the implementation: Primal Order is per-character (`player_characters.primal_order`), while the rest of Key Features comes from `classes.features` — per-class, shared by every Druid. It can't be spliced into that array without corrupting shared class data, so `buildClassFeaturesHtml(features)` now builds it as a separate prepended row (`primalOrderFeatureRowHtml()`, reading `CH.primal_order` directly) rather than trying to inject a synthetic string into the `features` array before mapping it. This also fixed a real ordering/timing gap the old `if (!Array.isArray(features) || !features.length) return ''` guard had: on the very first synchronous paint (before `CLASS_ROW` resolves), `features` is `null` and the old code returned nothing at all — now Primal Order alone renders on that first paint (`CH` is already populated by then), and the real class features join it once `CLASS_ROW` loads, with no flicker or missing-then-appearing item.
Icons reuse existing `FEAT_ICONS` glyphs rather than inventing new ones — sparkles (`&#10024;`, already used for "spellcasting"/"beast spells") for Magician, crossed swords (`&#9876;`, already used for "extra attack"/"fighting style"/smite features) for Warden. `PRIMAL_ORDER_TIPS` (the old tooltip-text-only lookup) replaced with `PRIMAL_ORDER` (icon + description per path, one object instead of two near-duplicate ones once the tooltip use was gone) — description text is the same real, paraphrased 2024 PHB mechanics from the prior entry, just with the "Magician:"/"Warden:" prefix dropped since the row's own name already says "Primal Order: Warden".
Verified live on Hana (real Druid, `primal_order:'Warden'`): "Primal Order: Warden" now renders as the first Key Features row with the crossed-swords icon and "You gain proficiency with martial weapons and medium armor.", positioned above the real Spellcasting/Wild Shape/Druidic/Druid Circle/Timeless Body/Beast Spells rows from `classes.features`, screenshotted. Confirmed the old Details-column note is gone (`cs-class-details` no longer contains `primal-order` anywhere). Confirmed Kael (Ranger, no `primal_order`) shows Key Features starting directly with Favored Enemy, unaffected. Zero console errors on both. `new Function()` syntax check clean; diff-scoped literal-emoji grep confirmed both new icon entities landed as `&#10024;`/`&#9876;` text, not re-normalized into literal glyphs by the Edit tool.

**2026-08-19 (new session, `anavale-wiki-class-features` worktree/branch — 3 small class-feature builds, closes 3 real `requests` rows) — `sheet/index.html`, `character.html`, `js/character.js`, `player_characters` schema.**
Ask covered 3 independent items; checked the `requests` table mid-task (per §1's own standing habit) and found real, matching DM entries for 2 of the 3 — "Kael: Pre-Partition Script language" and "Kael: Expertise in Survival" — which turned my earlier synthetic test picks (Caparian Deep / Perception) into the wrong real values; caught before finishing, corrected to the real ones. A third, "Hana: Warden path", confirmed the Primal Order ask's own framing was already the real fact, not a hypothetical.
**1 — Ranger's second language.** New `player_characters.language_extra_2` (text, nullable, same shape as `language_extra`). `sheet/index.html`: new `LANGUAGE_ROW_2`, fetched in `fetchLanguageData()` alongside the existing `language_extra`/Druidic fetches, rendered in `renderSpeaksBlock()` via the exact same row markup `language_extra` already uses (glyph/name/spoken_where, no tooltip) — purely "show it if it's set," no class check, per instruction. `character.html`: duplicated the language-card picker verbatim as a new `#char-language-section-2` (hidden by default), gated on `CHAR_STATE.draft.class_id === 'ranger'` via a new `updateSecondLanguageVisibility()`, called both from `selectClass()` (class is chosen in Stage 2, *after* the language section in Stage 1, so this has to be reactive) and from `restoreStage2Selections()` (fires every time Stage 1 is re-entered, covering "picked Ranger in Stage 2, came back to Stage 1"). Found and fixed a real bug surfaced by duplicating the card markup: `restoreStage2Selections()`'s primary-language restore used an unscoped `document.querySelectorAll('.char-lang-card')`, which — once a second section with the same class and the same `data-value`s existed — would incorrectly mark matching cards `.selected` in *both* sections at once; scoped both restores to their own `#char-language-section`/`#char-language-section-2` container. `buildCharacterEntry()` gains `language_extra_2`, gated on `d.class_id === 'ranger'` (not just the picker's own visibility) so a stale pick survives a class change back off Ranger. `js/character.js`'s dev-only `randomizeDraft()` left untouched — out of scope, not part of the real player flow. Kael backfilled to the real value (`pre-partition`), not the synthetic test pick — confirmed via `requests`.
**2 — Expertise.** New `player_characters.expertise_skills` (text[], nullable, default `'{}'` — same shape as `skills`). `computeSkillTotal()` now also checks `expertise_skills`, doubling `profBonus` only when the skill is *also* in `skills` (Expertise requires proficiency first — enforced in the check itself, not just documented as an invariant). Skill-row marker: for an Expertise skill, the "Prof." label is fully replaced (never shown alongside) by a brain icon (`&#129504;`, HTML entity per C2) + the shared `.cs-ab-tip` "?" circle, carrying its own `data-tip="Expertise doubles your proficiency bonus for this skill."` — the skill name's own, unrelated `SKILL_TIPS` tooltip is untouched (that's a different tooltip, for a different thing; conflating the two was the "old, confusing placement" the ask called out). Widened `.cs-ab-skill-marker` 26px→34px to fit both icons without crowding. Kael backfilled to the real value (Survival, matching the real `requests` row) rather than the synthetic Perception test pick.
**3 — Primal Order.** New `player_characters.primal_order` (text, nullable). Displayed as a small labeled note (`primalOrderNoteHtml()`) directly under the Class tab's Details column (Primary ability/Saves/Casting/Armor/Tools/Weapons) — real class info/traits, not a new location — shown only when set, no class gate (a non-Druid simply never has it set). "?" tooltip gives the real 2024 PHB mechanics, paraphrased rather than quoted verbatim (copyright — confirmed via 2 independent sources, CBR and a WebSearch synthesis, that Magician grants one extra Druid cantrip plus a Wisdom-mod-based bonus (min +1) to Intelligence (Arcana/Nature) checks, and Warden grants proficiency with martial weapons and medium armor; a third source's claims about shields and terrain-based bonus spells didn't independently corroborate and were dropped as likely conflating with the Circle of the Land subclass). Hana set to `Warden` — the real, DM-confirmed fact this session's own ask was built on, not a test value; matches the real `requests` row found mid-task.
Verified live end-to-end on Kael Evander and Hana Sable via a fresh local server (`8791` was already serving the `anavale-wiki` main worktree, not this one — confirmed via `curl`+`cwd` before touching anything, started a new one on `8820` instead, per the standing "may be serving another session" lesson). Kael: Speaks block shows Nombi Frost + Pre-Partition Script (2 rows, correct colors/sub-text); WIS card shows Survival with the brain+tooltip marker at `+7` (WIS mod +3, profBonus 2 doubled to 4) and Perception still plain `Prof.`/`+5`. Hana: Class tab shows "Primal Order: Warden" with the correct tooltip text, Druidic row unaffected. `character.html`'s new picker verified via direct JS calls (not just visual): `selectClass('ranger')` toggles the section visible immediately; picking cards in both sections keeps `.selected` correctly scoped to their own section (confirming the restore-bug fix); a full page reload with a saved draft correctly restores both cards' selected state and the section's visibility; `buildCharacterEntry()` correctly nulls `language_extra_2` for a non-Ranger draft carrying a stale `language_2` pick. Zero console errors throughout. `new Function()` syntax check clean on `sheet/index.html` (1 block), `character.html` (3 blocks), and `js/character.js`. Diff-scoped literal-emoji grep clean — the one new glyph (🧠) is the `&#129504;` entity, confirmed not silently re-normalized to a literal character by the Edit tool (a recurring risk on record in this file's own history). All 3 matching `requests` rows (`Kael: Pre-Partition Script language`, `Kael: Expertise in Survival`, `Hana: Warden path`) marked `done` with notes. A 4th, unrelated `requests` row surfaced in the same search (`Hana: free Speak with Animals spell`, `needs-decision`) was left untouched — not part of this ask.
DM QA'd and approved on the live local build; all 4 checks passed. Merged into `main` and pushed. Worktree removed per the standing worktree-hygiene rule (§1, Start item 6) once merged and clean.

**2026-08-19 (new session, `anavale-wiki-live-session` branch — 5 direct DM edits to the Session Command Center: Quick Actions removed, Inspiration control retired, two-column Live Session layout, Quick Note FAB scoped to the SCC page, Quests dropped from the Manage tab) — `dm.html`.**
All 5 items were real, already-tracked `requests` rows (`fa2250b7`/`dd959055`/`895c6924`/`33e593f2`/`2b979ea5`, area `scc-edits`) — confirmed via SQL before starting, all `not-started`, all updated to `done` at the end of this session as part of finishing the task.
**1 — Quick Actions removed.** Deleted the `.scc-live-quick-actions` strip card entirely — its "Go to Combat" shortcut and its own local "Quick Note" button/textarea (`sccLiveAddNote()`/`sccLiveSaveNote()`, a second POST path into `session_note_entries` distinct from the global FAB's `sccOpenQuickNoteModal()`/`sccSubmitQuickNote()`). Deleted both functions outright as dead code once their only caller was gone, rather than leave them unreferenced — the FAB already covers the identical need from every tab, so there's no narrower "local" note-capture worth keeping alongside it.
**2 — Inspiration retired from Live Session.** Confirmed it was a genuine full duplicate before touching it, not just visually similar: `sccLiveToggleInspiration()`/`sccLiveRenderInspiration()` read/wrote the exact same `character_sheet.has_inspiration` column `sheet/index.html`'s own `renderInspiration()` already toggles on the player-facing sheet — one real control, not two. Removed the strip card, both functions, both call sites (`sccLoadPlayers()`'s success callback, `sccRenderLivePanel()`), and the now-dead `.scc-live-inspiration-row/-token` CSS.
**3 — Two-column layout, plus a real ancestor-chain CSS bug found and fixed along the way.** `.scc-live-strip-wrap` is now a CSS Grid (`minmax(0,300px) minmax(0,1fr)`), left column = the existing `.scc-live-strip` cards (now a vertical stack, `position:sticky; top:1rem`), right column = the existing `.scc-live-accordion` — same sticky-sidebar technique `.scc-quest-two-col`/`.scc-quest-col-sidebar` already use for the Quests panel two sections over, not a new pattern, with the identical 900px stack-to-one-column breakpoint. Verifying this live surfaced a real, pre-existing bug that also silently affects that older `.scc-quest-col-sidebar` sticky element: `#scc-panel-live`/`#form-session.active`/`.dm-forms-panel`/`.dm-main` all declare `overflow:auto|hidden` as part of an app-shell-with-bounded-viewport layout that never actually engages in practice — `.dm-app` only sets `min-height:100vh`, not `height`, so the instant this panel's real content exceeds one viewport (true the moment any accordion section opens), the whole chain just grows to fit content instead of clipping, and the real document ends up scrolling — but `position:sticky` still binds to the nearest ancestor with a non-`visible` overflow regardless of whether that ancestor ever actually scrolls, so the strip was sticking (uselessly) within a box that never itself moves relative to the real page scroll. Fixed with 4 new rules scoped via `:has(#scc-panel-live.active)` (mirroring this file's own existing `:has()` scoping convention at line ~4632) so only the Live Session sub-panel gets the unclipped chain — Combat, Notes, and every other tab keep their original overflow contexts untouched. Confirmed via `getComputedStyle()` that the full ancestor chain now resolves to `overflow:visible` end-to-end.
**4 — Quick Note FAB scoped to the Session Command Center page.** `sccUpdateQuickNoteFabVisibility()` toggles `#scc-quicknote-fab`'s display based on `CURRENT_TAB === 'session'` (the tab whose own header literally reads "🎲 Session Command Center," holding Live Session/Combat/Notes) — called from inside `switchTab()` itself so it fires on every tab switch, including the initial boot-to-Manage call. Verified live: FAB hidden on Manage, visible on Session.
**5 — Quests removed from the Manage tab's entity list.** Confirmed the real reason first, not just followed the instruction blind: `cmEditEntry()` has no `#form-quest` to populate, so a quest row in Content Manager was listed but not actually editable from there — real quest editing already lives on the top-level Quests tab (the Quest Dashboard), untouched by this change. Removed `'quest'` from both `types` arrays that build `CM_ALL` (`cmRefresh()`, `cmRefreshFromArrays()`) and the `<option value="quest">` from the Type filter dropdown. `CM_ARRAYS.quest`/`CM_ARRAY_NAMES.quest`/`CM_ICONS.quest` left in place, harmlessly unused — they're just never reached anymore since nothing iterates `'quest'` from Manage code paths.
**Verification, all live against real Supabase data** (fresh local server, confirmed serving this session's edits via `curl` before trusting it, per the standing lesson): Session tab screenshotted showing the real two-column layout with real party data (Hana/Kael/Visenya) and a real expanded Quests accordion section ("Something Is Wrong With Gerald," real beats/DM notes) sitting beside the still-visible left-column strip; Manage tab confirmed via direct JS inspection that `CM_ALL` holds 0 quest rows against the real 10 rows in `QUESTS`, and the Type filter dropdown no longer offers a Quests option; FAB confirmed hidden (`display:none`) on Manage and visible (`display:flex`) on Session via computed style.
**Sticky positioning itself could not be end-to-end confirmed by this session's own tooling — flagged rather than claimed, per D8.** Both `window.scrollTo()`-driven scrolling on the real strip *and* on a completely isolated, freshly-injected sticky test `<div>` appended directly to `<body>` (zero ancestor-overflow involvement at all) failed to demonstrate any "catch" at `top:16px` — the element's `getBoundingClientRect().top` tracked scroll position in exact 1:1 lockstep in every case, and the pre-existing `.scc-quest-col-sidebar` sticky element (built and presumably verified working in an earlier session) reproduces the identical failure signature in this same environment, confirming it's a tool-level limitation and not something specific to this session's CSS. The real, ancestor-chain bug (item 3 above) was diagnosed and fixed through direct CSS-spec reasoning and confirmed via computed-style checks, not via visually watching it stick — that final visual confirmation needs a real browser. Asked the DM to do exactly that one check.

**2026-08-19 (new session, `anavale-wiki-sheet-langs` branch — Druidic language row + "Always Prepared" cantrip fix, DM-QA'd and merged to `main`) — `sheet/index.html`.**
Closed the two-part item flagged in the prior session's Next Priorities: (a) `renderSpeaksBlock()` only ever rendered `ch.language_extra`'s row, so a Druid's real, always-known Druidic language never showed up regardless of what they picked at creation. `fetchLanguageData(ch)` now runs a second, parallel fetch — `sb.from('languages').select('*').eq('id','druidic').maybeSingle()`, gated on `ch.class_id === 'druid'` — into a new `DRUIDIC_LANGUAGE_ROW`, and `renderSpeaksBlock()` renders it as a second `.cs-mi-speaks-row` right after the character's own chosen language, same glyph/name/spoken_where markup and per-language inline color. Its name carries a `.cs-ab-tip` "?" trigger sourced from the row's real `tip` column (a full 2024-rules explainer, already populated in Supabase — no new data needed), shown only when a `tip` value exists so older language rows without one don't grow a dead icon; `renderSpeaksBlock()` self-wires via `wireAllTooltips()` after render, matching the established pattern (`renderInspiration()`/`renderHitDice()`) for any function that introduces its own `[data-tip]` element. (b) `renderSpellsLearnedTable()` gave every row an interactive Prepared checkbox, including cantrips (`level === 0`) — misleading, since a cantrip is always prepared under 2024 rules and never needs a daily choice. Cantrip rows now render plain muted text ("Always Prepared", `var(--char-text-faint)`) instead of a checkbox; leveled spells (`level >= 1`) are completely unchanged.
Verified live against Hana Sable's real data (token `9fb5b3d283aa91594c2e186a517e51bf`, a real Druid with `language_extra:'jugabi-canopy'` and 2 real cantrips already learned) — a stale process was already listening on the usual `:8791` dev port serving content without these edits (confirmed via `curl | grep`, per the standing "may be serving another session" lesson), so verification ran against a fresh server on `:8799` instead, left `:8791` untouched. DOM read confirmed both Speaks rows render with distinct real colors and the Druidic tip wired (`_tipWired:true`); confirmed via `setActiveTab('class')` that Guidance/Produce Flame (both real cantrips) render "Always Prepared" with zero checkbox markup while Entangle/Faerie Fire/Goodberry/Healing Word (real level-1 spells) keep their real, working `<input type="checkbox">` controls unchanged. Screenshotted both states (header Speaks block, full Class tab) via the established tall-viewport fresh-navigate workaround for this environment's known scroll/paint glitch. Zero console errors. `new Function()` syntax check clean on the one extracted inline script block.
DM QA'd and approved on the live local build. One open issue surfaced during QA — a tooltip max-width bug — is a separate, already-logged issue, not part of this fix, not touched here. Merged into `main` and pushed; branch fast-forwarded onto `origin/main`'s one intervening CLAUDE.md-only commit first (no code conflict), then `main` fast-forwarded onto this branch — a straight line, no merge commit needed. Worktree removed per the standing worktree-hygiene rule (§1, Start item 6) once merged and clean.

**2026-08-19 (new session, `prepared_count_formula` correction — logging a fix made directly in Supabase, not through this file, plus a record-correction the search for it surfaced) — `classes` table, no code changes.**
Searched the Decision Log for an existing entry on this exact fix before writing one, per instruction — didn't find it. What the search *did* find, and what makes this worth flagging rather than just logging quietly: the 2026-08-16 "Class-tab spell system Round 1" session's own **Fix 3** entry (above, under that date) documents doing the opposite of what actually needs to be true. That entry describes rebuilding `prepared_count_formula` for **all 8** casting classes as `fixed_table`, on the claimed basis that "All 8 casting classes' prepared-spell count is now a flat per-level table" in the 2024 rules. Confirmed live against the real, current Supabase state before writing anything: `cleric`/`druid`/`wizard`/`paladin`/`ranger` are `ability_mod_plus_level` right now (paladin/ranger at `level_divisor:2, round:"down"`, the other three at `level_divisor:1`), while `bard`/`sorcerer`/`warlock` are `fixed_table` — the exact shape `computePreparedMax()` in `sheet/index.html` already supports for both types, and the exact shape the DM's fix (below) restored. So Fix 3's "all 8 as fixed_table" characterization was wrong — full and half casters do keep an ability-modifier-based Prepared Spells count in the real 2024 PHB; only known-spell casters (Bard/Sorcerer/Warlock, who never had a separate "spells known" count to begin with under 2024 rules) use a flat table. Fix 3's entry is left in place rather than edited/removed (append-only log), but this entry supersedes its "all 8 fixed_table" claim — a future read of Fix 3 should be checked against this one, not taken as current.
**The actual fix, applied directly in Supabase (not via a session here)**: `cleric`/`druid`/`wizard`/`paladin`/`ranger`'s `prepared_count_formula` were reset from `fixed_table` (with copy-pasted `values` tables, presumably carried over from the Bard/Sorcerer/Warlock shape during Fix 3) back to `ability_mod_plus_level`, matching the type `computePreparedMax()` was already built to handle. `bard`/`sorcerer`/`warlock` were checked against the 2024 PHB and correctly left on `fixed_table` — those three's flat tables were never wrong, only the other five's conversion to that same shape was.
Not independently re-verified against the 2024 PHB by this entry — taken on the strength of the live Supabase read (which matches the pre-Fix-3 Handoff B values `computePreparedMax()` was originally built and verified against, per the 2026-08-14 Handoff B entry) plus the fix's own stated reasoning. If this needs re-confirming against source later, the 2026-08-14 Handoff B entry has the original per-class WebFetch verification.

**2026-08-19 (continuing the same session — final broad sanity pass across the whole `anavale-wiki-scc-ux` branch before merging into `main`, one continuous simulated DM session touching Setup → Live Session → Combat → Notes → History/Audit Mode → Currency in order, not isolated re-checks) — `dm.html`.**

Explicit instruction: everything below had already been individually verified working across the prior rounds this session — the goal was to catch anything that only breaks from features interacting, by walking through a realistic continuous session rather than testing one feature at a time in isolation. It worked: found and fixed 2 real bugs, neither of which any of the many earlier isolated-verification rounds this session had surfaced.

**Method.** Real DOM interaction throughout — `element.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', {bubbles:true, cancelable:true, view:window}))` on a `document.querySelector`-located element (still exercises real onclick handlers and event bubbling, unlike calling the underlying JS function directly, while being immune to the coordinate-click drift that plagued earlier attempts this session) — with direct function calls reserved for exercising specific write-guard edge cases with no natural UI trigger in the test fixture. Real Supabase SQL reads before and after every consequential step, not assumed from UI state.

**Setup, Live Session — passed cleanly except for bug #1.** All 5 entity pickers and Prep Notes/Premise confirmed editable outside audit mode. All 5 Live Session accordion sections opened with real content. Cycled Gobblewump Gerald's relationship badge, clicked the Quests beat rail and confirmed the Current Beat widget updated, round-tripped Setup↔Live Session via "+ Add Scenario" with state preserved, used Quick Note and confirmed it landed as a real DM-Only Tagged Note — later confirmed correctly picked up by Copy Post-Session Summary in the Notes step, proving the two compose correctly across tabs.

**Bug #1 — `sccVendorRefreshLookup()` only ever refreshed the global lookup drawer, never the surface actually visible.** Found by exercising the Vendor/Shop feature from the Live Session Locations accordion instead of the lookup drawer it was originally built and verified against — a legitimate, Phase-3b-confirmed entry point, just not the one prior testing happened to use. The old code unconditionally called `sccOpenLookup()`, which (a) force-opened an unrelated drawer panel over whatever the DM was actually looking at, and (b) never refreshed the Locations-panel card that was really on screen, leaving it showing stale pre-action state (e.g. "Nothing in stock yet." immediately after adding real stock) until manually collapsed and reopened. Fixed by mirroring the exact "refresh whichever surface is actually showing this entity" pattern already established by `sccCycleRelationship2()`: check `SCC_LOOKUP_TYPE`/`SCC_LOOKUP_ID` before touching the drawer at all, always refresh `sccRenderLocationPanel()` and (if loaded) `sccFgDraw()` for the Field Guide sidebar. Verified live: added and removed a real Torch from Mira Pont's stock via the Locations accordion, confirmed the card updates in place with zero drawer intrusion either time. One pre-existing, minor rough edge surfaced but deliberately not chased further: `sccRenderLocationPanel()`'s full re-render collapses every card's expand state (no tracked JS state for it, only inline-style toggling) — a real but small UX cost of the fix (data now correct, one extra click to see it), not introduced by this change and out of this pass's scope to also resolve.

**Combat — passed cleanly, no issues.** Staged via both the search box and roster quick-add chips (including a 2x multi-quantity add). Confirmed `sccStartCombat()`'s real hide mechanism (individual element-ID `style.display` toggles, not a wrapper class — read the source before trusting an initial wrong check against `.scc-combat-builder`'s own computed style). Condition picker: applied Frightened to the correct combatant by array index (`data-for-idx` confirmed), real Supabase-backed condition data, visual tag + remove-on-click confirmed. Initiative reorder: moved the active combatant one slot — `SCC_TURN_IDX` correctly followed it, `.active-turn` class moved with it; moved two unrelated combatants — `SCC_TURN_IDX` correctly stayed put, confirming the swap-symmetric tracking logic holds for both cases in the same session, not just in isolation. Dropped Grusk Enforcer to 0 HP, awarded his real 15gp3sp7cp loot to Hana Sable specifically — confirmed her `gold_cp` rose by exactly 1537cp and his `loot` object zeroed. Ended combat with a second combatant's un-awarded 3gp still set — Combat Summary correctly showed only "3 GP" collected (proving the already-awarded, already-zeroed loot didn't double-count) and correctly split it 1gp each to all 3 players, confirmed via SQL. Builder controls reappeared post-End-Combat.

**Notes — passed cleanly, no issues.** Added a real Tagged Note directly from the tab (confirmed auto-focus + SQL). Filled all 5 recap textareas and Save Notes — confirmed the PATCH-if-exists fix from several rounds ago in this same session still holds against the real session-1 row. Copy Post-Session Summary confirmed containing all 5 fields plus both real Tagged Notes correctly grouped by category, including the untitled DM-Only note from the Live Session Quick Note step with no stray empty title line.

**History/Audit Mode — the highest-risk step, per its own explicit framing, and where bug #2 turned up.** Entered a locked synthetic session 999 via the established safe pattern (`SCC_ALL_SESSIONS` client-side push only, real Session 1 confirmed via SQL to still have exactly 1 row throughout). Setup's Premise/Prep Notes/Date confirmed genuinely readonly with correct synthetic text. All 5 Live Session sections rendered correct historical content. All 14 write functions gated in the earlier urgent-fix round — HP adjust/set, Add/Confirm/Remove Condition, Toggle Inspiration, relationship-cycle, beat-status-save, all 4 Vendor functions, both Field Guide editor functions — called directly against real targets and confirmed via SQL to be genuine no-ops (HP/conditions/inspiration byte-identical, beat 1 still `not_started`, `npc_relationships` unchanged, `vendor_stock` still 0 rows, `EDIT_MODE` stayed `false`), re-confirming the whole guard set holds together, not just individually. Combat correctly showed the audit-mode note, not a live tracker.

**Bug #2 — the exact scenario this step's own checklist asked to test.** Switching to the Notes tab while auditing (`sccSwitchPanel('notes')`) silently showed the *live* session's real Tagged Notes instead of the audited session's — with the correct readonly styling applied, so it looked entirely correct while showing the wrong session's data. Root cause: the `'notes'` branch called `sccRenderTaggedNotes()` with no argument, defaulting to `SCC_SESSION.session_number` — and `SCC_SESSION` deliberately never repoints away from the live session during audit view, the identical root cause behind the Scenarios bug and the Copy-Summary-header bug both already fixed earlier this same session. `sccLoadHistorySession(999)`'s own initial call correctly passes `999` and shows the right (empty) state — the bug only surfaces on the *next* tab switch, which is precisely why the earlier History-audit round (which only tested `sccLoadHistorySession()`'s own render, never a subsequent tab-away-and-back through normal nav) never caught it, and precisely the value of this pass's continuous-session design. Checked all 10 real call sites of `sccRenderTaggedNotes()` before touching anything: 8 pass no argument, but 7 of those only run when `SCC_VIEWING_HISTORY` is already `false` (session-complete reset, "Back to Current Session" restore, 2 write-triggered re-renders inside functions already gated by their own history check) — `sccSwitchPanel`'s was the one genuine gap, a pure navigation action reachable identically in either audit state. Fixed with the same `sccResolveViewedSession()` helper built for the Scenarios fix — `sccSwitchPanel('notes')` now resolves whichever session's actually being viewed via `#scc-session-number` before calling `sccRenderTaggedNotes()`. Verified via a fresh page reload (ruling out a stale-state artifact from the first test run) with a new synthetic session: Notes tab now correctly shows session 999's real (empty) state; re-confirmed the Setup↔Session round-trip (the bug fixed in the earlier urgent round) still holds after the reload; "Back to Current Session" confirmed full restoration; a real, deliberate 1-point HP edit outside audit mode confirmed the guards aren't over-blocking, reverted immediately via SQL.

**Currency — passed cleanly, no issues.** Edited Kael Evander's Gold/Silver/Copper to a distinct value (11/2/5) via the real DM Tools UI, confirmed `gold_cp` landed as exactly 1125, opened his real sheet on `main`'s local checkout (the only checkout with the read-only lockdown, per the immediately-prior round) via his real token, confirmed 11/2/5 displays correctly and all 3 fields are genuinely `readOnly:true`.

**Final revert and sweep, everything confirmed byte-identical to the true pre-pass baseline via direct SQL**: all 3 players' `gold_cp` (900/880/5400), HP (12/12/10, full), conditions/inspiration/death-saves (clean), `npc_relationships` (`{"oro":"ally"}`), quest beat 1 (`not_started`), all 5 session-1 recap fields (`null`), `session_note_entries` (0 rows), `vendor_stock` (0 rows), `inventory` (22 rows, untouched), `field_guide_sessions` session 1 (140 blocks, untouched). Zero console errors across the entire pass. Pre-push checks clean: raw parse, `new Function()` on all 7 inline script blocks, diff-scoped literal-emoji grep empty.

**Verdict: the branch is ready to merge into `main`.** 2 real bugs found — genuine feature-interaction bugs, exactly the class this pass exists to catch, neither surfaced by any of this session's earlier isolated-feature verification rounds — both fixed and re-verified live against real Supabase data. Every other checklist item across all 6 areas passed cleanly, including under the accumulated state changes of one continuous, realistic session.
