**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`, `dm.html`, `css/tokens.css`: Inventory tab built against the real Figma pass (`docs/inventory-tab-build-spec.md`, nodes 51:2260 desktop / 51:2265 mobile). New session, later same day as the entries below.**
Full build per the spec's 6-step order, plus one DM-requested correction after initial report-back.
**10-slot equip data model**: `EQUIPPED`/`SHEET.equipped` extended from 4 keys to 11 (`armor`, `shield`, `hand_right`, `hand_left` existing; `head`/`neck`/`earrings`/`arms`/`back`/`legs`/`feet` new) backing 10 visual doll positions (`EQUIP_DOLL_SLOTS`). Left Hand is the one doll position with two mutually exclusive backing keys (`hand_left` weapon vs. `shield`) — equipping either now auto-clears the other at that position (extended the existing two-handed-weapon lock pattern), and a two-handed main-hand weapon clears both. DM Tools' accessory Equip Slot dropdown fixed to the exact 7 new values (was offering `finger`/`wrist`/`waist`, which have no doll position, and missing `earrings`/`arms`/`back`). Checked the spec's suspected `equipSlotMap`-overrides-back-to-null bug in `generateItem()` — confirmed it does **not** exist; the category-specific block runs after the base entry and already sets `equip_slot` correctly from the dropdown.
**Doll visual**: built with CSS `grid-template-areas` echoing the humanoid layout rather than the Figma frame's literal absolute px positions — those broke the mobile frame outright (slots rendered off-canvas, confirmed via screenshot) and wouldn't reliably reflow on desktop either, since the design canvas width doesn't match any real container width in this responsive page. Mobile falls back to a wrapping flex grid (same slot visual, no silhouette), per the spec's own pre-approved mobile deviation. Mid-build, found real Figma-exported assets (`assets/images/character-sheet/equipped-gear/gear-slot.jpg` frame + `equipped-gear-silhouete.png` body silhouette) already staged untracked on disk — missed on the first pass (shipped a CSS-only approximation initially), caught before finalizing by checking `git status` for untracked files and correcting to use the real assets. Left three further staged-but-unused assets (`assets/images/ui/icon-x.svg`/`icon-search.svg`/`icon-arrow-dropdown.svg`) unwired — their fill color is baked-in dark, so dropping them in as-is against this page's dark theme would be a real contrast regression without a `mask-image` recolor pass; flagged rather than risked.
**Actions column — DM correction after first report-back**: initial build tucked the quantity input/edit/delete controls into the Description cell since Figma's 8 columns don't show a slot for them. DM correctly called this out as breaking the spec's own explicit decision that Description is flavor text only. Fixed: real 9th "Actions" column (`cs-inv-th-actions`/`cs-inv-td-actions`, 132px, centered), reusing the existing `.cs-inv-qty-input`/`.cs-inv-edit`/`.cs-inv-del` classes — no new styling invented. `colspan` on the loading/empty-results rows bumped 8→9, mobile table `min-width` fallback bumped 760px→860px. Re-verified on both characters/breakpoints after the fix.
**Select checkbox → bulk delete only**: no bulk action shown in the mockup; built as multi-select → "Delete selected" (bar appears above the table, reuses the existing single-item delete-confirm modal rather than a new one) since that's the standard reason a table gets row checkboxes — this was the spec's own documented default, not a fix, recorded here since it's part of what shipped.
**Favorites/drag-reorder dropped from this view**: no equivalent in the new table design. Removed `toggleFavorite()`, `wireInventoryDragDrop()`, `getDragAfterElement()`, `persistDragOrder()`, `toggleInvDesc()` entirely (dead code, not stubbed) — confirmed via grep that none are referenced elsewhere before deleting. `is_favorite`/`sort_order` columns themselves are untouched and still written on insert (stable ordering, just no longer player-reorderable here); `is_favorite` also stays live and unrelated on the Notes tab (`.cs-inv-section-label` CSS class is shared with Notes' Pinned/Notes group labels — kept for that reason even though Inventory's own favorites grouping that originally used it is gone).
**Currency/Carrying/+Add Item/filter**: currency rebuilt as 3 real pill inputs (Gold/Silver/Copper) recomputing `gold_cp` on blur, replacing the old single "Set Gold" flow. Carrying bar added (STR×15 capacity — verified 195lb on Kael real STR 13, 210lb on Hana real STR 14), track/fill using `--blue-700`/`--blue-300` per the spec's prose (the raw Figma export read the opposite way, but prose was unambiguous and this is the conventional progress-bar reading). `--blue-300` was genuinely missing from `tokens.css` despite the spec's token table claiming 50–800 all existed — added it. +Add Item modal relocates the existing catalog search unchanged behind a new trigger button. New own-inventory Search/Category/Search-button row filters the already-loaded `INVENTORY` client-side (click-to-search, distinct from the catalog search's live-filter). Category list (Accessory/Armor/Consumable/Misc/Shield/Tool/Weapon) verified against real `items.category` via direct SQL — exactly 7 distinct values, matching the spec exactly. That check also surfaced that the *old*, unchanged-and-relocated catalog-search dropdown was already missing "Misc" (10 real items use it) — added it there too since already touching that exact `<select>`.
**Flagging again, third time now**: the Figma pull for this Inventory frame reuses the same MainInfo header component (`51:1385`) and once again shows a Temp HP bar with a `/8` denominator. This is the third time this exact placeholder has surfaced in a Figma pull for this component this session (cosmetic pass, MainInfo regression-fix pass, now here) — each time correctly identified as mockup filler data, not a real design change, and left alone per the standing decision that there's no real `hp_temp_max` column to show a bar/proportion against. Recording this explicitly so it reads as a known recurring artifact of this Figma component rather than something to re-investigate from scratch next time it shows up.
Verification: JS syntax parsed clean via `new Function()` on the extracted inline script block (same check throughout, both before and after the Actions-column fix). Zero console errors on Kael Evander (real leather armor + longbow equipped) and Hana Sable (fully empty `equipped: {}`) at both 1600px desktop and 375px mobile. Confirmed the item table's `overflow-x:auto` wrapper contains its own horizontal scroll without the page itself overflowing (`document.body.scrollWidth === window.innerWidth`, both 378). All 5 tabs (Your Story/Ability Scores/Inventory/Class/Notes) still switch correctly post-change. Not bundled with any other tab's work per the DM's explicit instruction.

**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`: fixed a real orphaned-tooltip bug (condition pill's own remove button); gave the Moral Alignment card the shared 5-alignments illustration as its thumbnail. Commits `9d0660e`, `c1c808b`, same session as the two regression-fix batches above.**
DM reported a stuck tooltip bubble screenshot (hover text for "Invisible" still on screen after interacting with conditions) plus, mid-investigation, a second report that the Moral Alignment card's "Pragmatic" value has no icon/thumbnail like its sibling cards do.
**Tooltip bug — root cause found via D2, fixed in one shot per D3**: `wireTooltip()` shows a bubble on `mouseenter` and only removes it on `mouseleave` from the source element, both listeners bound once per element. The condition pill's "×" button lives *inside* the pill span that carries `data-tip` — clicking it never fires `mouseleave` (the mouse never leaves the pill's bounds to reach a child), and the click's handler (`removeCondition()` → `saveSheet()` → `renderConditions()`) immediately replaces the whole conditions row via `innerHTML`, destroying the pill without the event that would have cleaned up its bubble ever firing. The bubble, parented to `document.body` (not the pill), is orphaned permanently. Fixed centrally in `wireAllTooltips()` — which every re-render on the page already calls immediately after rebuilding its DOM — by sweeping and removing any stray `.cs-tooltip` elements before rewiring. Reasoned this is safe with a single mouse cursor: a legitimately-open tooltip on some *other*, unrelated element would already have received its own `mouseleave` before any click could reach a different part of the page, so the only case where a bubble survives to see this sweep is exactly the bug case (element destroyed while still hovered). Verified by scripting the exact repro (`mouseenter` then `.click()` on the pill's own remove button, no `mouseleave` in between) — bubble count went 1→0 across the click — and separately confirmed normal hover-then-actually-leave behavior is untouched (1→0 on a real `mouseleave` too, on the AC badge). Test mutation (removing then re-adding "Invisible" on Kael) reverted and confirmed via a follow-up SQL read.
**"Pragmatic" thumbnail — D7 correction**: first reported this as an unresolved asset gap ("no per-alignment icon exists, needs a DM decision") after confirming `character.html`'s 5 `.char-alignment-card` elements have no `data-img`/`background-image`/icon of any kind, and that the only alignment-related asset in the repo, `assets/images/img-alignment.webp`, is one combined illustration of all 5 alignments together rather than 5 separate per-value icons. That factual read was correct, but the conclusion was wrong — the DM corrected it on the spot: they'd already decided this combined image *is* the intended thumbnail, used as-is (same image regardless of which of the 5 alignments the character has), not cropped per-value like Gigglegloom Affinity/Home Region/Background's real per-value icons. Fixed per D7 (stopped, corrected the record, then wrote one fix): `alignCard = storyCard(...)` in `renderBackgroundCardsHtml()` now passes `'../assets/images/img-alignment.webp'` as `imgSrc` unconditionally, no per-alignment branching. Verified the image loads (`naturalWidth:1024`, `complete:true`) and renders inside the existing 75×75 `object-fit:cover` thumbnail frame correctly on Kael, zero console errors.

**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`: batch 2 of the regression fix pass — Your Story section-title size, Your Background card stretching, tooltip icon styling, "Why You Left" label color. Commit `6761c42`, right after the batch-1 MainInfo fixes (`0a81614`).**
Four more items from the same live-page-vs-mockup comparison as the batch-1 entry above, fixed and verified as a separate batch per the DM's explicit request (screenshot before/after each item, commit each batch separately). Used the `git stash` before/after pattern (established earlier this session) to get a clean "before" state for screenshots after realizing the first edit (section titles) had already landed before any screenshot was taken — stashed, reloaded or Kael to capture "before," restored the stashed change by hand (a plain `git stash pop` refused because the working tree already had the batch's other 3 edits in it by that point — re-applied the 2-line diff manually from `git stash show -p` instead of fighting the pop, then dropped the stash) rather than losing it.
**Section titles**: `.cs-ov-acc-title` (shared by all three "Your Story" headings) was `font-size: var(--text-4xl)` = 48px — should have been 32px per spec. Changed to `var(--char-ov-h3)` (already a 2rem/32px token, used elsewhere for exactly this kind of heading) and added explicit `font-weight:700` (Cinzel Decorative only ships as 700 anyway, but making it explicit removes any doubt). Also removed a now-obsolete mobile override (`font-size: 2.25rem` at max-width:600px) that existed only to shrink the old 48px down for narrow screens — at the new 32px default it's already smaller than that override was, so keeping it would have made mobile headings *bigger* than desktop.
**Your Background card stretching**: `.cs-story-cards` (the shared flex row for both Your Background's and Your Past's card grids) had no `align-items` set, so it defaulted to `stretch` — every card in a row was forced to the height of the tallest one, which is why Gigglegloom Affinity/Home Region/Moral Alignment (a few lines of text) were rendering with a huge dead zone at the bottom to match the Background card's real height (it has a whole Benefits box). Fixed with one line, `align-items: flex-start`, on the shared class — confirmed via `getBoundingClientRect()` that the four Your Background cards now render at four different real heights (204/228/208/497px on Kael) instead of all matching the tallest.
**Tooltip icons**: `.cs-ab-tip` (the shared "?" class used by the Background card's Benefits box, every Your Past card's Benefits box, and separately the Ability Scores tab's per-ability tooltips — three call sites, not two) was plain gold text with no circle/background at all, despite looking superficially like it might be styled. Rebuilt as a real 13×13px circle (`border-radius:50%`, `background:var(--gold)`, `color:var(--blue-800)`, bold, centered) — both `--gold` (#f0b429) and `--blue-800` (#0a0a0f) already matched the DM's spec values exactly, no new tokens needed. Verified via a DOM sweep, not just one card: all 15 instances on Kael's Your Story tab (Background + 5 Your Past cards with benefits) render identically, and separately confirmed the Ability Scores tab's 6 tooltips (one per ability) picked up the same fix automatically since it's the same shared class.
**"Why You Left" quest label**: `.cs-story-quest-label` had `color: var(--gold)` — a real mismatch with the spec, which reserves gold (`--currency-gold-coin`, a different token, used by `.cs-story-benefit-label` for "Benefits:") for Benefits-box labels only. Changed to `var(--text)` (#e8e8f0, matches spec exactly) — "Personal quest:" now renders as bold body-color text, matching the rest of that card, not mistaken for a second Benefits-style label.
Verification: DOM-measured (not just visually eyeballed) on Kael Evander — computed `font-size`/`font-weight`/`font-family` on all three section titles (32px/700/Cinzel Decorative), computed `color`/`font-weight` on the quest label (rgb(232,232,240)/700), computed size/bg/color/radius on 5 sample tooltip icons (13×13/gold/dark/50%), and real per-card heights in the Your Background row. Full spot-check repeated on Visenya Oryn (different background/past answers — Flamerage/Sohot/Measured vs. Kael's Featherflow/Nombi/Pragmatic) confirming none of the fixes are Kael-specific. Zero console errors on both characters throughout.

**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`: fixed 5 MainInfo regressions found by comparing the live page directly against the real Figma header (node `51:1385`) — duplicate logo, AC badge not stacking, HP bar too small, Speaks block wrong structure, tab bar not full width. Commit `0a81614`.**
DM flagged these against a mockup (no image was actually attached to the request — pulled the real Figma frame instead, same source used for the ground-truth rebuild the entry above this one). Loaded the live page (Kael Evander, local dev server on :8791) before touching any code, per D2/C1, and confirmed all 5 were real, not stale-cache artifacts from last session's `tokens.css` caching issue (used a `?cb=` query-string bust each reload).
**Duplicate logo**: root cause was a deliberate choice from the ground-truth rebuild session, recorded in that session's own comment ("Duplicates the sticky nav's logo deliberately — this is a real Figma component distinct from the site-wide nav bar, not a redundant copy of it"). The DM's read of the mockup overrides that — the real Figma frame for this page shows exactly one logo, in the PageTitle component, with no separate nav-bar logo at all. Removed `.cs-nav-logo`/`.cs-nav-title` (markup + CSS) entirely; `.cs-nav` now holds only "← Return to Wiki", right-aligned (`justify-content: flex-end`). Updated the now-false comment above `sheetPageTitleHtml` that claimed the duplication was intentional.
**AC badge**: `.cs-mi-ac-badge` was `display:inline-flex` with no `flex-direction` set, defaulting to row — "AC" and "14" were laid out side by side despite the comment above the rule correctly describing the intended stacked look. This was a real gap between what a prior session's computed-style check confirmed and what the CSS actually declared — the rule never had `flex-direction:column` to begin with. Fixed: `flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center;` plus `line-height:1` on both the label and number spans (their default line-heights were adding visible gap between the two lines that the tight Figma stacking doesn't have). Verified via `get_metadata` on the real Figma node — label and number are true stacked/centered elements, confirming the direction of the fix, not just its visual plausibility.
**HP bar**: was a fixed `120px × 10px` sliver. Pulled `get_design_context` on the real Figma bar node (`55:5981`) — it's `border-radius:999px`, height **37px** (full row height, matching the input boxes beside it), width flexible (fills whatever space is left in a 564px-wide row after the label/inputs/fraction text). Implemented as `.cs-mi-hp-slot` growing (`flex:1 1 320px`, was `0 0 auto`) so `.cs-mi-hp-compact-bar` (now `flex:1 1 auto; height:37px`) has room to expand into. Also fixed a real, separate, lower-priority violation of this file's own CSS rule (§8, "no raw hex outside `:root`") caught while touching this rule anyway — the bar's unfilled-track color was a bare `#371212`; added `--red-track` to `tokens.css` and pointed the rule at it. The fill color (`--red-strong`) was already correct — confirmed it matches the Figma fill hex (`#d62b2b`) exactly, no change needed there.
**Found but deliberately NOT changed, flagging for a DM decision**: the real Figma frame's Temp HP block also has a bar + "/ max" fraction (bicolor, cyan `#0d9ace`/`#042937`, same structure as the HP bar) — but this session's earlier cosmetic pass explicitly built and then *reverted* a Temp HP bar on the DM's own instruction, on the grounds that "5e has no real max temp HP to show a bar or proportion against," landing on a plain number-only badge instead (recorded twice in the Decision Log above). Since there's no `hp_temp_max` column and no data model for it, and the DM's own prior call was deliberate rather than a mistake, I did not reinstate a Temp HP bar just because the Figma frame happens to show one — that would silently reverse a discussed decision rather than execute the batch of fixes actually asked for. Left Temp HP as a plain input, unchanged. Flagging the conflict here rather than picking a side.
**Speaks block**: markup nested the "spoken where" sub-text inside the same flex column as the language name, with the rune glyph as a sibling centered (via `align-items:center`) against that whole two-line column — so the glyph visually landed at the seam between the two lines instead of next to the name, and the sub-text was indented to start after the glyph rather than spanning full width. Restructured to match the real Figma layout: a `.cs-mi-speaks-row` (glyph + name, inline) as one row, `.cs-mi-speaks-sub` as a second, separate, unindented full-width row below it. Also checked whether "Noto Sans Runic" (named in the DM's ask) is loaded anywhere in the codebase — it isn't, in this file or any other (`character.js`/`character.html`/`dm.html`), so there's nothing to "fix" there; it was never wired in. The rune glyphs (e.g. `ᚱᚢᚾ`) do render as real glyph shapes, not tofu/placeholder boxes — confirmed by temporarily blowing the glyph up to 80px in the live DOM and screenshotting it — via the browser's own system-font fallback for the Runic Unicode block, not a font this codebase declares. Left as-is since it's visually correct; noting the "Noto Sans Runic" name doesn't correspond to anything actually in the repo, in case the DM wants that font declared explicitly for consistency across browsers/OSes rather than relying on fallback.
**Tab bar width**: `.cs-tabbar` was rendered as a direct child of `.cs-wrap` (the page's narrow, 820px-capped default container), while literally every other section on the page (MainInfo header, page titles, Who You Are / Your Background / Your Past accordions, Ability Scores cards) already renders inside the wide `.cs-ov-breakout > .cs-ov-inner` wrapper built for the Aug 7 Overview redesign. The tab bar was the one piece never migrated into it, which is why it visibly sat inset ~290px per side relative to everything else at this viewport. Fixed by wrapping `tabBarHtml` in the same breakout markup — no new CSS needed, just adopting the existing pattern.
Verification: full reload cycle (cache-busted query string each time, not relying on HMR) against Kael Evander for all 5 fixes with DOM `getBoundingClientRect()`/`getComputedStyle()` checks (not just visual screenshots) confirming exact pixel results — AC badge `flex-direction:column` confirmed computed, HP bar 557×37px pill confirmed, Speaks row/sub rects confirmed non-overlapping and unindented, tab bar `x:20 w:1360` confirmed matching the Who You Are card's own rect exactly. Spot-checked Visenya Oryn (different species/HP-at-full/multi-glyph Conclave Script language) — bar renders correctly fully green... fully red-filled at 10/10 HP, multi-character glyph row renders correctly. Checked mobile viewport (375px) — HP bar and tab bar both reflow correctly, no overlap or overflow. Zero console errors throughout on both characters, both viewports. Committed but **not yet pushed** — awaiting DM confirmation before it goes live on GitHub Pages.

**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`: added the Inspiration badge's tooltip; fixed a real tooltip-clipping bug it surfaced (shared `wireTooltip()`, not Inspiration-specific).**
DM asked a direct status question — did the Inspiration badge get its tooltip when it was rebuilt during the header-alignment fix? No: `renderInspiration()` never set `data-tip` on the badge at all. Added it, verbatim DM-specified text: "Spend before any d20 roll — roll twice, take the higher result. You can only hold one at a time." Also added a `wireAllTooltips()` call inside `renderInspiration()` itself — every other `render*()` function that introduces `data-tip` elements this session already does this internally (`renderConditions()`, `renderClassTabDynamic()`, etc.); `renderInspiration()` was the one holdout, previously relying on whichever caller happened to run `wireAllTooltips()` afterward, which `toggleInspiration()` never did.
Verifying the fix (hover screenshot, not just a DOM `_tipWired` check — screenshots catch what DOM inspection can't) surfaced a second, real bug: the tooltip bubble rendered but its text ran off the right edge of the browser window, unreadable. Root cause: `wireTooltip()`'s positioning (`tipEl.style.left = rect.left + rect.width/2`, centered via CSS `transform: translate(-50%, ...)`) has never accounted for viewport edges — it just happened not to matter until now, because no `data-tip` element had ever sat this close to a screen edge before. The Inspiration badge does, by design, per the header-alignment fix (top-right corner of the whole header). Fixed in `wireTooltip()` itself, not special-cased per element: after appending the bubble to the DOM (so `offsetWidth` is measurable), clamp its center so the rendered bubble can't come within 8px of either viewport edge. This is a shared fix, not an Inspiration-only patch — every `data-tip` element in the file benefits from it, and the clamp is a no-op for anything not already near an edge (confirmed via the clamp math: `Math.max(half+8, Math.min(maxLeft, left))` only changes `left` when the unclamped value would already be out of bounds).
Verification: reloaded, confirmed via DOM inspection the badge carries the exact requested `data-tip` text and `_tipWired: true`. Hovered it and screenshotted — full tooltip text now visible and fully on-screen (previously cut off mid-sentence at the viewport edge). Spot-checked no console errors. Did not exhaustively re-verify every other tooltip in the file pixel-by-pixel, but the clamp logic is provably a no-op for centered elements away from an edge, so this isn't a speculative "should be fine" — it's a direct consequence of the math.

**2026-08-09 — `sheet/index.html`: Conditions free-text input replaced with a real picker sourced from the `conditions` Supabase table, matching the `dm.html` SCC fix from earlier the same session.**
DM asked a direct status question first — did the sheet's own Conditions UI get updated when `dm.html`'s Live Session panel was fixed to pull from Supabase? No: the MainInfo header's Conditions row (built in this session's tab-shell slice) has been free text since it shipped — type anything, one generic purple pill style for every entry, no tooltip. That slice's own CSS comment said as much at the time ("conditions are arbitrary free text so there's no per-name color source, kept to one consistent accent rather than inventing one") — true when written, no longer true now that a real `conditions` table exists.
Implementation mirrors the `dm.html` fix's pattern exactly, per the DM's explicit instruction to use it as the reference: `fetchConditionsData()` (`sb.from('conditions').select('*').order('sort_order')`) added to the same init `Promise.all` that already fetches `backgrounds`/`regions`/`species`/`languages`, populating a new `CONDITIONS_DATA` global — fetch-once-at-boot, same shape as `dm.html`'s `sccInit()` → `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA`, just using this file's own `sb.from()` Supabase-client style rather than `dm.html`'s raw-REST `sccFetch()` helper (different file, different existing convention, same underlying pattern). `renderConditions()` rewritten: the free-text input + Add flow is now a `<select>` populated from `CONDITIONS_DATA` filtered to conditions not already applied (so a character can't double-add the same condition, and the picker naturally shrinks as more are added); each pill sets `--cond-bg`/`--cond-border`/`--cond-text` inline from the matching row's real columns (same CSS-custom-property technique `dm.html`'s SCC panel uses) and a real `data-tip` from the row's `tip` column, wired through `wireAllTooltips()` (the tooltip sweep built earlier this session) — conditions get real hover tooltips for the first time, not just color. Updated the CSS comment that was now factually wrong instead of leaving it to mislead the next read. `CONDITIONS_DATA` came back with 18 rows (all of `dm.html`'s 17-entry `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` plus `Exhaustion`, which was never added to the DM picker list) — the sheet's picker is a straight reflection of the whole table, not a hand-maintained list, so it didn't need its own separate "add Muted" step at all.
Testing surfaced a pre-existing, unrelated latent race: calling `removeCondition()` twice with zero delay between them (only possible via direct function calls in a test script, not real clicks) can let two `saveSheet()` PATCH requests complete out of order and leave stale data — confirmed this doesn't happen under normal click-driven use (any perceptible gap between actions resolves in order), and the fire-and-forget `saveSheet()` pattern itself predates this change (the old free-text `addCondition()`/`removeCondition()` had the identical risk). Not fixed — out of scope for what was asked, flagged in Current Focus as a known risk rather than silently left undiscovered.
Verification: full add/remove cycle on Kael Evander with a real wait between each action (not the zero-delay pattern that produced the race above) — added Charmed, confirmed via direct SQL read it persisted, removed it, confirmed via a second SQL read it cleared correctly. Separately added Muted + Poisoned together, confirmed via DOM inspection both pills carry correct distinct real colors and tooltips (`_tipWired: true`), confirmed the picker dropdown excludes both once applied, then removed both and confirmed the DB read clean before moving on. Clean spot-check on Visenya Oryn (fresh page load, zero console errors, `CONDITIONS_DATA` populated, no residual conditions). Before/after screenshots captured by stashing the change (`git stash`), screenshotting the live old free-text-input state, then `git stash pop` to restore — not by mentally reconstructing what "before" looked like.

**2026-08-09 — `dm.html`: added "Muted" to the SCC Live Session conditions picker; fixed a real gap this surfaced (Fading Stage 1/Color-Drained silently missing their real tooltip/color) rather than adding a third hardcoded copy of the data.**
Ask was narrow — add `'Muted'` to `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` (16→17 entries) so it's selectable in the Live Session conditions picker. Read `dm.html` before touching it (C1) and found the picker's actual color/tooltip source, `sccLiveRenderConditions()`'s local `COND_DATA` object, only covers the 15 core D&D conditions (Blinded through Unconscious) — `'Fading Stage 1'` and `'Color-Drained'`, both already in `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` before this change, have no entry there and were silently falling through to `sccLiveRenderConditions()`'s generic-fallback branch (flat purple pill, `tip: c` — just the condition's own name, no real rules text). Confirmed via SQL that a `conditions` table exists in Supabase (`color_bg`/`color_border`/`color_text`/`tip`/`is_custom` columns, seeded same-day per `created_at`) with all 18 conditions including Muted (`is_custom:true`, added ~8 minutes after the other 17 per its own `created_at`) — real data, not something to guess at.
Fix: added `sccFetch('conditions', '&order=sort_order.asc')` to `sccInit()`'s existing `Promise.all` (same fetch-once-at-boot pattern already used for `species`/`items`/etc.), populating `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA`. Explicitly left alone: a second, unrelated `COND_DATA` object exists in `sccPromptCondition()` (the Combat tab's "Add Condition" modal, ~line 12421) — same 15-condition hardcoded shape, doesn't reference `SCC_CONDITIONS_LIST` at all, and wasn't mentioned in the ask. Different feature (combat initiative tracker vs. Live Session panel), left untouched.
Verification: loaded `dm.html` locally (password gate is a plaintext client-side check, `anavale-dm` — not a real secret, this is the DM's own local dev tool), confirmed `window.SCC_CONDITIONS_DATA` populated with 18 rows including Muted, confirmed the "Add Condition" picker dropdown lists Muted as its own `<option>`. Added Muted + Fading Stage 1 + Poisoned to a test player (Hana), confirmed via DOM inspection that all three chips render with distinct correct colors and full real tooltips (Muted/Fading Stage 1 from the new Supabase lookup, Poisoned unchanged from local `COND_DATA` — correct precedence, local table still wins when a condition exists in both). Screenshotted the rendered chips and the picker dropdown. Cleared the test player's conditions afterward — confirmed via grep that `SCC_LIVE_CONDITIONS` is pure client-side in-memory state (`var SCC_LIVE_CONDITIONS = {}`), never written to Supabase anywhere, so no database cleanup was needed beyond resetting the JS variable and re-rendering.

Speaks lost its `--char-card-bg-subtle` box/border entirely so it reads as a plain column like the other two, not a distinct card — glyph+name+sub layout inside is otherwise unchanged. `renderSpeciesDesc()`/`renderSpeaksBlock()` async logic untouched, only their containing markup moved.
AC badge, HP bar, Temp HP badge, and the death-save swap were explicitly named by the DM as already matching — none of their markup, CSS, or JS logic was touched; `.cs-mi-vitals` now holds only the AC badge (Inspiration's removal from it is the only change to that row).

**2026-08-09 — Spell Slots stopgap (Class tab); `hp_temp` write-path audit; build-spec doc located as living outside this repo. Same session as the three entries below.**
**hp_temp audit** (DM asked to rule out a code bug, not just flag-and-move-on, after Kael's `hp_temp` read 5 unprompted between two check-ins this session): grepped every file that could plausibly touch it. Result — `hp_temp` is written in exactly one place in the whole codebase, `setTempHP()` in `sheet/index.html`, and only behind an explicit user click on "Set" (`parseInt(input.value)`, bails on `NaN`/negative, no default). `js/hp-calc.js` (`computeHpBase`/`reconcileHp`) never references `hp_temp` at all — confirmed by grep, not assumption. Cross-checked against this session's own tool-call history and never once interacted with the Temp HP input/Set button while testing. Conclusion: no write-path bug — production Supabase is the same database regardless of whether `sheet/index.html` is served from the local dev server or the real deployed site, so the most likely explanation is the DM or Kael's own player testing independently on the live site during this session. Left the value alone; no basis to treat it as wrong.
**Spell Slots stopgap**: DM's call, explicit and bounded — "simplest possible functional tracker... no new visual design beyond matching existing card tokens... mark it clearly as a temporary stopgap, not the real redesign... keep it minimal, don't polish." Built current/max pips for spell levels 1–9 in a new card on the Class tab (`renderSpellSlots()`/`toggleSpellSlot()`/`setSpellSlotMax()`), wired to `character_sheet.spell_slots`/`spell_slots_max`. Both columns were real but empty (`{}`) on all 3 characters — since nothing had ever written to them, there was no existing shape to match, so I defined the simplest one: `{ "1": 2, "2": 1, ... }`, level → count. Pip interaction mirrors the death-save pips exactly (click a filled pip to spend it and everything after; click an empty one to restore up to it) — same pattern, not reinvented. No per-class default slot tables and no "recommended max for your level" — purely a manual editor, per "don't polish." Verified end-to-end on Visenya (Warlock): set Level 1 max to 2, confirmed 2 pips render, spent one down to 1 filled, confirmed the write landed via a direct SQL read (`spell_slots: {"1":1}, spell_slots_max: {"1":2}`), then reverted both columns back to `{}` — no real character data should show invented values I made up for a UI test.

Searched the repo for it first rather than assuming — it isn't a file here (`docs/` has only `character-sheet-redesign-handoff.md` from Aug 7 and the unrelated project-plan doc). It lives in the DM's Claude Chat project knowledge, which Claude Code has no write access to per this file's own Tool Roles table. Handed the corrected text back to the DM to apply there instead of inventing a repo file for it.

**2026-08-09 — Character Sheet Tab Redesign v1: Class tab (third slice, same session as the two entries below). Two build-spec inaccuracies found and flagged rather than silently worked around.**
Moved the class summary card (image/name/level + Primary/Saves/Casting/Armor/Tools/Weapons) out of Your Story's Overview accordion into the real `#cs-tab-class` content (`buildClassTabHtml()`), same relocation pattern as the Ability Scores slice. Added a Hit Die row (new — `classes.hit_die` wasn't displayed anywhere on the sheet before) and `?` tooltips on Hit Die/Saves/Weapons only (`hit_die_tip`/`saves_tip`/`weapons_tip`), reusing the `.cs-ab-tip` element/style built for Ability Scores rather than inventing a second tooltip-icon pattern. Primary and Armor stay tooltip-free, matching `character.html`'s `.char-class-trait` exactly, per the spec's explicit "don't invent tips for them."
Key Features: ported `FEAT_ICONS` verbatim from `character.js`'s `renderClassGrid()` (~49 regex→icon pairs plus fallback; literal emoji converted to `&#decimal;` entities per C2, since this file's convention forbids literal emoji in JS strings). Wrote a new `parseClassFeature()` — split on `" — "` into name/description, then strip `" subclass at level N"` or `" at level N"` off whichever string currently holds the name (handles both bare entries like "Ranger Archetype subclass at level 3" and combined ones like "Otherworldly Patron subclass at level 1 — your patron defines..." where the level phrase sits on the name side of the dash). Verified this against all 72 real feature strings pulled from Supabase (not the character.js mirror, and not just eyeballed — ran it through Node) before writing any HTML around it; every entry parsed cleanly, including the trickiest dash+subclass+level combination. Alternate-row shading is pure `.cs-class-feature-row:nth-child(even)` CSS against a new `--blue-500` token — pulled the real hex (`#28304a`) from the Figma file itself via `get_variable_defs` on node `52:2705` rather than guessing or interpolating between the two blues already in tokens.css.
Two things the build spec got wrong, caught by verifying against real code/data instead of trusting the doc (CLAUDE.md's standing "if what you find conflicts with project knowledge, stop and say so" rule): (1) the spec says Key Features' icon+level parsing is derived "via the same pattern the character creator already uses" — false; `character.js` only assigns an icon and renders the raw string whole, it has never split name/description/level apart. Only the icon table was an actual port; the parser is new code written for this session, just conforming to the algorithm the spec described. (2) the spec says there are "66 features... 7 of the 66" bare subclass entries — real count from Supabase is 72 total (12 classes × 6, not 11 × 6) with 8 bare entries, not 7 (missed Wizard's "Arcane Tradition subclass at level 2"). Neither error changes anything about the implementation since the parser handles all 72 correctly regardless of what the spec counted, but both are now on record so the spec doc itself doesn't keep getting cited as ground truth on these two points.
Confirmed NOT building, not a silent gap: the spec describes the Class tab's Spell Slots section as relocating "existing `character_sheet.spell_slots`/`spell_slots_max` UI... as-is, no redesign yet." Grepped the entire codebase (`sheet/index.html`, `dm.html`, `character.html`, `js/character.js`) for `spell_slots` — zero matches anywhere. Both Supabase columns exist (confirmed via `information_schema`), but no UI was ever built to read or write them, on this sheet or anywhere else. There is nothing to relocate. Building one from scratch is real design work outside "no redesign yet" and needs a product call, not an engineering guess — left undone, flagged here and in Current Focus rather than either skipping silently or inventing a design unprompted.
Verification: full-page screenshots (not DOM-only — the DM specifically asked for this after the Ability Scores slice hit a Browser-pane rendering glitch on scroll) against two different classes on two real characters — Kael Evander (Ranger: mostly non-subclass dash entries plus one bare subclass line) and Visenya Oryn (Warlock: every subclass-tier feature is the combined dash+level case) — both rendered correctly, tooltips confirmed wired via DOM inspection (`_tipWired: true` on all three). The earlier screenshot glitch didn't recur once each check used a fresh tab and a tall (1400×2200) viewport instead of scrolling a fixed one; noted as the working pattern for future full-page checks in this environment, not a code issue. No Supabase writes in this slice (read-only display), so no test-data cleanup was needed.

**2026-08-09 — Character Sheet Tab Redesign v1: tab shell + shared MainInfo header (first slice). `js/hp-calc.js` confirmed real and committed.**
Built per the new Character Sheet Tab Redesign v1 build spec (Figma `55:5629`), scoped to exactly two pieces per DM instruction: the 4-tab shell (Your Story / Ability Scores / Inventory / Class — `ACTIVE_TAB` state, click handlers, show/hide, no data changes) and the shared `MainInfo` header (Figma `51:1385`, built once via `buildMainInfoHtml()` and reused above all four tabs rather than duplicated per tab). Only Your Story carries real content this pass — everything that already existed on the page moved into its content block unchanged; the other three tabs render a "coming soon" placeholder until their own build slices land.
Header additions, each backed by real data: species paragraph (`species.description`, populated 12/12 in the Aug 9 Supabase session below), Speaks block (new `languages` table, joined on `language_extra` — glyph/name/spoken_where), Conditions (+Add/remove-×, `character_sheet.conditions`), Inspiration toggle (`has_inspiration`), Temp HP as a plain badge that hides at 0/null (5e has no max temp HP), and a death-save pip tracker (`death_save_successes/failures`) that swaps into the HP bar's slot at 0 HP rather than appending below it. AC badge and HP bar are relocations of existing logic — `computeAC()`/`computeWeaponDamageHtml()` factored out of the old `renderEquippedAC()` so the math isn't duplicated between the header and the (still-present) Equipped section; HP math in `adjustHP()`/`setMaxHPModifier()`/`setTempHP()` is unchanged, only its render target moved.
Bug found and fixed during required browser verification, not in the original spec: the death-save view had no HP control at all, so a character at 0 HP had no way to ever heal back up through the sheet. Fixed by adding a minimal heal input next to the pips (`applyHPDelta()`, unchanged); crossing back above 0 now also resets both death-save counts to 0, matching the 5e rule and required for the bar/death-save swap to make sense the next time HP hits 0.
`js/hp-calc.js`: found untracked at session start, not mentioned anywhere in this log. Traced it fully before touching anything HP-related (per the DM's explicit ask) — confirmed via `git diff` on `dm.html`/`sheet/index.html` that it's a real, load-bearing shared module (`computeHpBase`/`reconcileHp`) extracted from logic that used to live duplicated inline in `sheet/index.html`, referenced by `<script src>` in both files, and actively called by both. Verified live against Supabase (not assumed) that every column it touches — `hp_con_mod_cached`, `hp_temp`, `hp_max_modifier`, `conditions`, `has_inspiration`, `death_save_successes/failures` — already exists, so nothing here is blocked on a pending migration. Root cause of it being untracked: a prior session did the refactor but ended without running `git add`/commit or writing a Decision Log entry. Committed tonight together with `dm.html`'s matching `reconcileHp()` call in `sccLoadPlayers()` (SCC player bar), since neither file works correctly without `hp-calc.js` and the two files' changes are one coherent unit.
Verification: live end-to-end against Kael Evander's real Supabase data, including a full HP→0→death-save→heal-back cycle (confirmed pip persistence, swap-back, and the reset via direct SQL read), then all test mutations (conditions, inspiration, HP) reverted to original values, confirmed via a second SQL read. Cross-checked Speaks/species rendering against Visenya Oryn (different species/language) as a read-only spot check.
Two things found but explicitly NOT touched, flagged instead: (1) pre-existing appearance-content duplication — `appearance_prompt` (when set) already restates the same fields the Appearance-section chips show, and a third version exists in the Overview summary card; relocating the paragraph into the header made the redundant copies more visually distant, not more redundant — left for the real Your Story tab build. (2) `docs/character-sheet-redesign-handoff.md` and `.claude/launch.json` are both still untracked from the Aug 7 session (the latter despite that session's Decision Log entry claiming it was "added") — unrelated to tonight's work, left uncommitted pending a separate decision.

**2026-08-08 — Full character-creator field audit: no further data-loss bugs found; 3 dead fields identified.**
After four data-loss bugs found one at a time this session (six `past_*` fields, then class skills), did a systematic pass instead of waiting for the next one to surface by accident. Enumerated every `<input>`/`<select>`/`<textarea>`/checkbox in `character.html` (grep-verified complete), traced each into `CHAR_STATE.draft`, confirmed every key `buildCharacterEntry()` reads matches a real `player_characters` column in both directions (no orphaned keys either way), then ran a live test — one fully-populated draft covering every field, through the real `buildCharacterEntry()`, 32 assertions, all passed. Result: the class-skills fix was the last one: every other field across all 5 stages is confirmed correctly reaching Supabase.
Found 3 unrelated dead fields — different failure mode, not data loss, since no UI ever collected them so there's nothing to have lost: `player_email` (no email input exists anywhere in the creator), `alignment_trait` (`selectAlignmentTrait()` and `.char-alignment-trait` exist in `js/character.js` but nothing in `character.html` ever calls/renders them — looks like a per-alignment-card sub-choice that was designed but never wired up), `appearance_data.glasses` (collected by `collectAppearanceData()`, but no `app-glasses` element exists). All three are always empty/null for every character, past and future, until someone builds the missing UI — not a bug fix candidate, a product decision if ever wanted.

**2026-08-08 — Fourth data-loss bug: class skill proficiencies never persisted.**
DM noticed Kael Evander's `skills` array was empty in Supabase despite ranger requiring 3 skill picks. Confirmed `validateStage(2)` in `js/character.js` blocks progression until `skills_count` skills are chosen, so this wasn't skipped — same failure class as the three `past_*` bugs (data collected correctly in the draft, never reaches the DB), but a different specific mechanism: `CHAR_STATE.draft['skills_' + classId]` (e.g. `skills_ranger`) is correctly read by the live preview, Stage 3 panel, and Stage 2 validation, but `buildCharacterEntry()` never read it at all — not a wrong-key typo, a missing field entirely. Confirmed systemic: Hana and Visenya also have `skills: []`. Also confirmed `player_characters.skills` is a fully dead column — not read anywhere in `sheet/index.html` or `dm.html` either, so this had zero visible symptom on the live sheet.
Fix: `buildCharacterEntry()` now writes `skills: d.class_id ? (d['skills_' + d.class_id] || []) : []`. Applies to all future characters.
Recoverability (checked, not guessed): none. Unlike "Your Past," the specific skill checkboxes chosen live only in the browser's `localStorage` draft, which `submitCharacter()` deletes on success — no server-side snapshot exists at any point, in the DB, the GitHub-dispatch payload, or anywhere else. This happened at Session Zero (2026-07-06); nothing to query now. Only path to recovery is asking each player directly what they picked (same remediation as "Your Past") — DM has not yet decided whether to re-collect. Per-character options if they do: Kael (Ranger, choose 3): Animal Handling, Athletics, Insight, Investigation, Nature, Perception, Stealth, Survival. Hana (Druid, choose 2): Arcana, Animal Handling, Insight, Medicine, Nature, Perception, Religion, Survival. Visenya (Warlock, choose 2): Arcana, Deception, History, Intimidation, Investigation, Nature, Religion.

**2026-08-08 — UI clarity fix; "Your Past" fully backfilled from DM-recollected answers; second starting-gold bug found and fixed.**
UI: `dm.html`'s pending-items "Mark Added" button read as if it might auto-grant the item. Renamed to "I Added This" with a `title` tooltip spelling out that it's a manual checklist clear, not a grant — matches dm.html's existing pattern of pairing action buttons with `title=` hints.
Your Past backfill: the DM re-collected all six previously-lost answers directly from Hana, Kael, and Visenya. Matched each to its exact `data-value` from `character.html`'s option cards (not guessed), cross-checked against each character's already-correct `organization_joined` as a sanity check. Applied the resulting mechanical effects via the existing `PAST_EFFECTS`/`computeSkillBonuses`/`computeFinalAbilityScores` system from the entry above — each character's already-applied organization bonus was left untouched, only the newly-unlocked `raised`/`friend` effects were added on top. Verified every number via the real live functions in a local browser (fed each character's actual stored data through `computeSkillBonuses()`, not hand-derived) before writing to Supabase, then re-verified the live sheet rendered the final values correctly for all three.
Gold bug #2 (found during the required pre-fix audit, not previously known): `sheet/index.html`'s `BG_GOLD` table — used to seed `character_sheet.gold_cp` on first sheet load — was a flat 50gp/75gp placeholder completely disconnected from `ANAVALE_BACKGROUNDS[bg].starting_gold` in `js/character.js` (the real 5–25gp PHB-style values shown to players during creation). Combined with the already-known "Stage 4 shopping spend never subtracted" bug, every character's seeded gold was significantly higher than intended, regardless of shopping. Audited all 5 creation stages (class, species, ability scores, gear, review) confirmed no other stage affects starting gold — only background `starting_gold` and Stage 4 shopping do, plus the imagined-past `merchant-guild` org bonus (already correctly isolated). Also confirmed: adding an inventory item on the live sheet never auto-deducts `gold_cp` anywhere in the codebase — this path doesn't exist yet, so there's nothing to reconcile there today.
Fix: renamed `BG_GOLD` → `BG_STARTING_GOLD` with the real per-background values (mirrored from `character.js`, same pattern as `FEAT_TIPS`). New `player_characters.starting_gold_spent_cp` column, written by `buildCharacterEntry()` as `calcGoldSpent()` at submission (that function only exists in `character.js`'s live DOM-driven form — the persisted value lets `sheet/index.html` use it without duplicating the shopping-cost logic). Seed formula is now `(real background gold) − starting_gold_spent_cp + starting_gold_bonus_cp`, applies to all future characters.
Backfill: DM confirmed none of the three characters have real play history on their gold yet (Kael's low balance was a manual DM test value, not session spending) — so all three got a clean direct set to their true correct amount, verified via the same live-function replay used for the original gold audit: Hana 900cp (9gp), Kael 880cp (8.8gp), Visenya 5400cp (54gp, preserving her already-correct +50gp merchant-guild bonus).

**2026-08-08 — Fixed "Your Past" data-loss bug; built imagined-past mechanical effects; backfilled 3 existing characters.**
Bug: `collectStage1Data()` in `js/character.js` stores the seven "Your Past" answers as `past_raised`/`past_friend`/`past_pet`/`past_love`/`past_org`/`past_left-behind`/`past_why-left`, but `buildCharacterEntry()` read them from `who_raised_you`/`dearest_friend`/etc., which were never set — every submitted character silently lost six of seven answers (confirmed via Supabase: all 3 real PCs had identical null pattern). Same wrong-key bug also found and fixed in two preview-only functions (`renderSummaryCard()`, `renderStage3Panel()`) that had never shown their "+1 skill from imagined past" chips as a result.
Feature: the "Your Past" cards' effect text (e.g. "+1 Wisdom, +1 History proficiency", "+50gp starting gold") was preview-only — nothing applied it. Built `PAST_EFFECTS` (single source of truth in `js/character.js`, replacing the old `PAST_SKILL_LOOKUP`/`PAST_SKILL_GRANTS` dicts, which disagreed with each other and with the card copy — `kind-parents` now correctly grants Insight) plus shared helpers `computeFinalAbilityScores()`, `computeSkillBonuses()`, `computeStartingGoldBonus()`, `computeDmPendingItems()`, `computeFeats()`, used by both the live creator preview and `buildCharacterEntry()` so they can't drift. New `player_characters` columns: `skill_bonuses` (flat +1s, explicitly not full proficiency — matches existing tooltip copy), `starting_gold_bonus_cp`, `dm_pending_items`, `feats`. `ability_scores` now stores the *final* value (raw + background bonus + imagined-past bonus) at submission — previously background bonuses were computed live in two duplicate places and never persisted or reached `sheet/index.html` at all; this also fixes that for all future characters. `sheet/index.html`'s `fetchOrCreateSheet()` now folds `starting_gold_bonus_cp` into the initial `gold_cp` seed and adds the Tough feat's +2 HP/level to the initial `hp_max` calc. New "mentor" friend-answer skill choice (History/Medicine) built as a picker modeled directly on the existing kept-to-myself org picker (`friend-mentor-skill-picker` in `character.html`, `selectMentorSkill()` in `character.js`). Minimal DM-facing "pending items" list added to `dm.html`'s SCC player bar (`sccMarkPendingItemAdded()`) for DM-add items like "Wanderkeep Field Kit" — nothing auto-grants these.
Backfill (DM-approved, one-time): Hana Sable, Kael Evander, Visenya Oryn each got their background ability bonus + feat applied (all 3 are the entire `player_characters` table) and their surviving `organization_joined` answer's skill bonus/feat/DM-item applied — the other six lost past-answers were NOT guessed and remain unset until each player re-picks. Visenya's merchant-guild "+50gp" was added on top of her current `character_sheet.gold_cp` (mid-campaign, not a reset). Hana's `hp_max`/`hp_current` recomputed +2 for Tough. All values verified end-to-end in a local browser session (no test data written to production) before the real Supabase writes.
Sheet display (same session, requested as a follow-up before this was considered done): `sheet/index.html` now shows all three new fields, since storing them correctly isn't enough if a player can't see them. `skill_bonuses` → new "Bonus skills" row in the existing Your Past story block (`.cs-story-row`, no new CSS). `feats` → new chip list (`.cs-feat-chip`, new CSS) in the Ability Scores accordion, each with its rules text from a new `FEAT_TIPS` lookup mirrored from `character.js` (sheet doesn't load that file — same mirroring pattern as `ANAVALE_BACKGROUNDS`/`ALIGNMENT_PHB`/etc.). `dm_pending_items` → new "Pending From Your DM" section reusing the existing `.cs-gear-item`/`.cs-gear-grid` styling, positioned right after Starting Gear, only rendered when non-empty. Verified against all 3 real characters' actual post-backfill data (Hana: Tough chip + HP tooltip text, +1 Athletics, "Common weapon of choice"; Kael: Alert chip, +1 Survival, "Wanderkeep Field Kit"; Visenya: Alert chip, +1 Persuasion, no pending section, 100gp).

**2026-08-07 — Character sheet redesign: Overview + Ability Scores (docs/character-sheet-redesign-handoff.md).**
Implemented per Figma `P0tcVgf02XeI13Tz0TtRaX` (36:310 desktop / 36:435 mobile). Replaced `cs-hero`/`cs-type-banner`/most of `cs-identity-grid` in `sheet/index.html` with the new Overview (portrait+money+weight card, name/pills/blurb, alignment/background/gigglegloom cards, class card) and Ability Scores accordions — DM confirmed replace-not-duplicate, wide breakout container scoped to just these two sections, photo upload kept. New tokens added to `css/tokens.css` only where no existing token matched. Currency (cp/sp/gp inputs) and the weight bar both wired to existing `gold_cp`/inventory-sum logic, verified live against real Supabase data (token test against "Kael Evander"). Class card reads a new `classes` table that doesn't exist yet — renders placeholders until the migration (drafted, schema in handoff §6, tools/casting_summary content awaiting DM sign-off) is approved and run. `generateSummary()` in `js/character.js` rewritten to the six-paragraph narrative spec in handoff §8 (gendered pronouns, organic length, empty fields omitted) — required changing `#char-auto-summary` from `<p>` to `<div>` in `character.html` to hold multiple paragraphs. Found but did not fix (pre-existing, out of scope): species-portrait 404 (`sheet/index.html` portraitSrc uses the wrong filename pattern — real files are `sp-{species}-{suffix}.png` not `{species}-{suffix}.webp`), a duplicate `.char-summary-body` CSS rule in `character.css`, and stale `WHY_LEFT_LABELS`/`RAISED_LABELS` option values in `sheet/index.html`'s untouched "Your Past" section. **2026-07-23 — Equipped section added to sheet/index.html.**
New Equipped section (armor/shield/main hand/off hand) rendered between Gold and Inventory in `renderCharacter()`, populated live via `renderEquipped()` (called from `renderLivePlay()` alongside `renderHP()`). Reads/writes `character_sheet.equipped` jsonb; item stats fetched live from `items` by id into `EQUIPPED_ITEMS`. AC computed dynamically in `renderEquippedAC()`: base 10, armor `base_ac` + dex-by-weight-class (heavy = none, medium = capped at `max_dex_bonus` ?? 2, light/none = full dex), shield `ac_bonus` (default 2), plus `magic_bonus` from armor/shield. New equip-search modal injected into `#cs-modal-root` (never inside `.cs-wrap`, per the modal rule), filtered by category per slot. All new symbols use HTML entities (`&#8230;`, `&#10005;`), not literal emoji — existing literal emoji elsewhere in the file (📝, 🗺, etc.) predate this and were left alone. HEAD after push: `be4ecec`.
